Conclusion: RoboPhD的Elo锦标赛选择方法在多数复杂任务上表现最优，特别是在需要从简单种子智能体发展为复杂多策略系统的场景中。研究将RoboPhD作为多功能工具包以MIT许可证发布，提供简单的optimize_anything() API用于进化各种复杂智能体。

Abstract: 2026 has brought an explosion of interest in LLM-guided evolution of agentic artifacts, with systems like GEPA and Autoresearch demonstrating that LLMs can iteratively improve prompts, code, and agent architectures across diverse domains. As adoption accelerates, a central question emerges: given the same information, the same seed agent, and the same objective, which optimization algorithm yields the best results under the same evaluation budget? This question becomes critical when evaluations are expensive, such as when they require human judgment or multiple LLM calls.
  We present the first systematic comparison of three optimization paradigms -- Elo tournament selection (RoboPhD), Pareto-based selection (GEPA), and greedy hill-climbing (Autoresearch) -- across four benchmarks spanning abstract reasoning, cloud scheduling, SQL generation, and financial QA, all under a fixed budget of 1,500 evaluations. RoboPhD introduces validation-free evolution: instead of splitting the budget between training and validation, it uses Elo competition on training data to simultaneously evaluate agents and drive evolution. All three systems receive seed agents with diagnostic print() statements that evolution can grow, enabling self-instrumenting agents that develop increasingly informative diagnostics for the benefit of their evolutionary successors.
  Using a single default configuration, RoboPhD outperforms both GEPA and Autoresearch on three of four benchmarks, losing only on the simplest task, where the winning solution (from our Autoresearch adaptation) required under 90 lines of code. On ARC-AGI, RoboPhD evolves a 22-line seed agent into a 1,013-line multi-strategy system, improving accuracy from 27.8% to 65.8% using Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite as the solver. We release RoboPhD as a versatile toolkit under the MIT license with a simple optimize_anything() API for evolving diverse complex agents.

</details>


### [115] [REAM: Merging Improves Pruning of Experts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04356)
*Saurav Jha,Maryam Hashemzadeh,Ali Saheb Pasand,Ali Parviz,Min-Joong Lee,Boris Knyazev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REAM是一种新的MoE LLM压缩方法，通过分组合并专家权重而非删除专家，在保持原始性能的同时减少内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型MoE LLM模型参数达数百亿，部署面临显著内存挑战。传统方法如权重剪枝和量化存在局限性，REAP方法通过剪枝专家会损失性能，需要更好的压缩方案。

Method: 提出Router-weighted Expert Activation Merging (REAM)方法，不删除专家而是将专家分组并合并其权重，更好地保留原始模型性能。通过控制校准数据混合（通用、数学、编程数据）来探索性能权衡的帕累托前沿。

Result: REAM在多个MoE LLM模型上评估，在多样化多项选择题和生成式基准测试中，通常优于REAP和其他基线方法，在许多情况下与原始未压缩模型性能相当。发现MC和GEN性能之间存在权衡，该权衡取决于校准数据的混合。

Conclusion: REAM是一种有效的MoE LLM压缩方法，通过专家权重合并而非删除，在减少内存需求的同时更好地保持模型性能，为大型MoE模型部署提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLMs) are among the top-performing architectures. The largest models, often with hundreds of billions of parameters, pose significant memory challenges for deployment. Traditional approaches to reduce memory requirements include weight pruning and quantization. Motivated by the Router-weighted Expert Activation Pruning (REAP) that prunes experts, we propose a novel method, Router-weighted Expert Activation Merging (REAM). Instead of removing experts, REAM groups them and merges their weights, better preserving original performance. We evaluate REAM against REAP and other baselines across multiple MoE LLMs on diverse multiple-choice (MC) question answering and generative (GEN) benchmarks. Our results reveal a trade-off between MC and GEN performance that depends on the mix of calibration data. By controlling the mix of general, math and coding data, we examine the Pareto frontier of this trade-off and show that REAM often outperforms the baselines and in many cases is comparable to the original uncompressed models.

</details>


### [116] [Decocted Experience Improves Test-Time Inference in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04373)
*Maohao Shen,Kaiwen Zha,Zexue He,Zhang-Wei Hong,Siru Ouyang,J. Jon Ryu,Prasanna Sattigeri,Suhas Diggavi,Gregory Wornell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文作为提升LLM性能的补充扩展维度，通过"提炼经验"构建更好的输入来指导推理，相比单纯增加推理时间计算更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过增加推理时间计算（如更长推理、采样或搜索）来提升LLM性能的方法，在复杂推理和智能体任务中成本高昂且可能导致次优探索。需要探索更高效的性能提升途径。

Method: 提出将上下文作为扩展维度，系统研究如何通过"经验"构建更好的输入来指导推理。核心是"提炼经验"机制：从经验中提取精华、组织成连贯结构、检索关键信息以构建有效上下文。研究经验增强智能体，分析如何从经验中推导上下文、性能如何随经验积累扩展、良好上下文的特征以及支持上下文构建的最佳数据结构。

Result: 在数学推理、网页浏览和软件工程等推理和智能体任务中验证了提炼经验作为有效上下文构建关键机制的有效性。展示了性能如何随积累的经验扩展。

Conclusion: 上下文是提升LLM性能的重要扩展维度，提炼经验是构建有效上下文的关键机制。相比单纯增加推理时间计算，基于经验的上下文构建能更高效地指导复杂推理和智能体任务。

Abstract: There is growing interest in improving LLMs without updating model parameters. One well-established direction is test-time scaling, where increased inference-time computation (e.g., longer reasoning, sampling, or search) is used to improve performance. However, for complex reasoning and agentic tasks, naively scaling test-time compute can substantially increase cost and still lead to wasted budget on suboptimal exploration. In this paper, we explore \emph{context} as a complementary scaling axis for improving LLM performance, and systematically study how to construct better inputs that guide reasoning through \emph{experience}. We show that effective context construction critically depends on \emph{decocted experience}. We present a detailed analysis of experience-augmented agents, studying how to derive context from experience, how performance scales with accumulated experience, what characterizes good context, and which data structures best support context construction. We identify \emph{decocted experience} as a key mechanism for effective context construction: extracting essence from experience, organizing it coherently, and retrieving salient information to build effective context. We validate our findings across reasoning and agentic tasks, including math reasoning, web browsing, and software engineering.

</details>


### [117] [Automatically Generating Hard Math Problems from Hypothesis-Driven Error Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04386)
*Jiayu Fu,Mourad Heddaya,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种新的数学基准生成流程，利用AI生成假设来识别LLM在特定数学概念和技能上的弱点，并针对这些弱点生成新的基准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准大多依赖人工构建，难以扩展，无法跟上LLM发展速度，且难以提供新实例来缓解过拟合问题。现有自动生成方法很少关注识别LLM在具体数学概念和技能上的错误倾向，且大多只能生成特定类别的基准。

Method: 提出新的数学基准生成流程：1）使用AI生成假设来识别LLM在哪些具体数学概念和技能上表现不佳；2）基于这些弱点生成针对性的基准问题。该方法具有高度适应性，可扩展到数学以外的领域。

Result: 实验表明假设准确性与生成问题难度正相关：基于最准确假设生成的问题将Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct的准确率降至45%，而原始MATH基准上的准确率为77%。该流程具有高度适应性，可应用于数学以外的广泛领域。

Conclusion: 提出的基准生成流程能有效识别LLM弱点并生成针对性测试问题，是一种有价值的工具，可用于探索LLM在不同领域的能力表现，解决了现有基准难以扩展和针对性不足的问题。

Abstract: Numerous math benchmarks exist to evaluate LLMs' mathematical capabilities. However, most involve extensive manual effort and are difficult to scale. Consequently, they cannot keep pace with LLM development or easily provide new instances to mitigate overfitting. Some researchers have proposed automatic benchmark generation methods, but few focus on identifying the specific math concepts and skills on which LLMs are error-prone, and most can only generate category-specific benchmarks. To address these limitations, we propose a new math benchmark generation pipeline that uses AI-generated hypotheses to identify the specific math concepts and skills that LLMs struggle with, and then generates new benchmark problems targeting these weaknesses. Experiments show that hypothesis accuracy positively correlates with the difficulty of the generated problems: problems generated from the most accurate hypotheses reduce Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct's accuracy to as low as 45%, compared to 77% on the original MATH benchmark. Furthermore, our pipeline is highly adaptable and can be applied beyond math to explore a wide range of LLM capabilities, making it a valuable tool for investigating how LLMs perform across different domains.

</details>


### [118] [GUIDE: Interpretable GUI Agent Evaluation via Hierarchical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04399)
*Yuwen Zhai,Runze Li,Liang Wang,Nian Shi,Liwu Xu,Wei Zhang,Ran Lin,Bo Xu,Benlei Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GUIDE框架通过将GUI智能体轨迹评估分解为轨迹分割、子任务诊断和总体汇总三阶段，解决长轨迹评估的准确性和可解释性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体评估方法采用对整个动作-观察序列的单一整体判断，在长时程任务上不可靠且仅提供二元判断，缺乏对失败原因和位置的洞察，限制了评估作为智能体开发诊断工具的效用。

Method: GUIDE框架包含三个顺序阶段：1)轨迹分割将完整轨迹划分为语义连贯的子任务单元；2)子任务诊断在上下文中评估每个单元，分配完成状态并生成结构化错误分析和纠正建议；3)总体汇总将各子任务诊断聚合为任务级判断。

Result: 在三个基准测试中验证：工业电子商务数据集(932条轨迹)、AGENTREWARDBENCH(5个网页智能体任务，1302条轨迹)和AndroidBench。GUIDE显著优于现有评估器，比最强基线准确率高出5.35个百分点，同时生成可直接指导智能体改进的结构化诊断报告。

Conclusion: GUIDE通过操作有界子任务段而非完整轨迹，缓解了随着任务复杂性增长而降低现有评估器性能的上下文过载问题，提供了准确且可解释的GUI智能体评估框架。

Abstract: Evaluating GUI agents presents a distinct challenge: trajectories are long, visually grounded, and open-ended, yet evaluation must be both accurate and interpretable. Existing approaches typically apply a single holistic judgment over the entire action-observation sequence-a strategy that proves unreliable on long-horizon tasks and yields binary verdicts offering no insight into where or why an agent fails. This opacity limits the utility of evaluation as a diagnostic tool for agent development. We introduce GUIDE (GUI Understanding and Interpretable Diagnostic Evaluation), a framework that decomposes trajectory assessment into three sequential stages mirroring the compositional structure of GUI tasks. Trajectory Segmentation partitions the full trace into semantically coherent subtask units. Subtask Diagnosis evaluates each unit in context, assigning a completion verdict and generating a structured error analysis with corrective recommendations. Overall Summary aggregates per-subtask diagnoses into a task-level judgment. By operating on bounded subtask segments rather than full trajectories, GUIDE mitigates the context overload that degrades existing evaluators as task complexity grows. We validate GUIDE on three benchmarks: an industrial e-commerce dataset of 932 trajectories, AGENTREWARDBENCH spanning five web agent tasks with 1302 trajectories, and AndroidBench for mobile device control. Across all settings, GUIDE substantially outperforms existing evaluators-achieving up to 5.35 percentage points higher accuracy than the strongest baseline-while producing structured diagnostic reports that directly inform agent improvement.

</details>


### [119] [MolDA: Molecular Understanding and Generation via Large Language Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04403)
*Seohyeon Shin,HanJun Choi,Jun-Hyung Park,Hongkook Kim,Mansu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MolDA：一种基于掩码扩散的新型多模态分子语言模型，用离散大语言扩散模型替代传统自回归架构，通过双向迭代去噪实现全局结构一致性和化学有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归架构的多模态分子模型存在局限性：严格从左到右的归纳偏差不适合生成化学有效分子，难以处理非局部全局约束（如环闭合），且在序列生成过程中容易累积结构错误

Method: 1) 使用离散大语言扩散模型替代传统自回归主干；2) 采用混合图编码器提取全面的结构表示，捕捉局部和全局拓扑；3) 通过Q-Former将结构表示对齐到语言标记空间；4) 为掩码扩散专门数学重构分子结构偏好优化；5) 通过双向迭代去噪确保全局结构一致性

Result: MolDA在分子生成、描述和性质预测任务中展现出全局结构一致性、化学有效性和鲁棒推理能力

Conclusion: 掩码扩散模型相比自回归架构更适合分子生成任务，能更好地处理全局约束和确保化学有效性，为分子发现提供了新的多模态框架

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced molecular discovery, but existing multimodal molecular architectures fundamentally rely on autoregressive (AR) backbones. This strict left-to-right inductive bias is sub-optimal for generating chemically valid molecules, as it struggles to account for non-local global constraints (e.g., ring closures) and often accumulates structural errors during sequential generation. To address these limitations, we propose MolDA (Molecular language model with masked Diffusion with mAsking), a novel multimodal framework that replaces the conventional AR backbone with a discrete Large Language Diffusion Model. MolDA extracts comprehensive structural representations using a hybrid graph encoder, which captures both local and global topologies, and aligns them into the language token space via a Q-Former. Furthermore, we mathematically reformulate Molecular Structure Preference Optimization specifically for the masked diffusion. Through bidirectional iterative denoising, MolDA ensures global structural coherence, chemical validity, and robust reasoning across molecule generation, captioning, and property prediction.

</details>


### [120] [Empirical Characterization of Rationale Stability Under Controlled Perturbations for Explainable Pattern Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04456)
*Abu Noman Md Sakib,Zhensen Wang,Merjulah Roby,Zijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种评估模型解释一致性的新指标，通过量化相同标签输入的SHAP值余弦相似度来检测模型解释的不一致行为，并在情感分析数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释AI评估大多停留在实例层面，未能明确量化相同类别或微小输入变化下归因模式的一致性。可靠的模式识别系统应在相似输入上表现一致，其解释也应保持稳定。

Method: 提出基于余弦相似度的解释一致性指标，使用预训练的BERT模型在SST-2情感分析数据集上实现，并扩展到RoBERTa、DistilBERT和IMDB数据集进行鲁棒性测试。应用SHAP计算特征重要性，量化相同标签输入的SHAP值相似度。

Result: 通过一系列实验评估了该指标识别错位预测和模型解释不一致的能力。与标准保真度指标相比，新指标能有效识别模型行为偏离预期目标的情况，支持更稳健的模型行为评估。

Conclusion: 该框架通过量化模型对相似输入是否依赖一致的归因模式，为构建可信AI系统提供了更深入的理解和更稳健的验证方法，支持实际模式识别管道中更稳健的模型行为评估。

Abstract: Reliable pattern recognition systems should exhibit consistent behavior across similar inputs, and their explanations should remain stable. However, most Explainable AI evaluations remain instance centric and do not explicitly quantify whether attribution patterns are consistent across samples that share the same class or represent small variations of the same input. In this work, we propose a novel metric aimed at assessing the consistency of model explanations, ensuring that models consistently reflect the intended objectives and consistency under label-preserving perturbations. We implement this metric using a pre-trained BERT model on the SST-2 sentiment analysis dataset, with additional robustness tests on RoBERTa, DistilBERT, and IMDB, applying SHAP to compute feature importance for various test samples. The proposed metric quantifies the cosine similarity of SHAP values for inputs with the same label, aiming to detect inconsistent behaviors, such as biased reliance on certain features or failure to maintain consistent reasoning for similar predictions. Through a series of experiments, we evaluate the ability of this metric to identify misaligned predictions and inconsistencies in model explanations. These experiments are compared against standard fidelity metrics to assess whether the new metric can effectively identify when a model's behavior deviates from its intended objectives. The proposed framework provides a deeper understanding of model behavior by enabling more robust verification of rationale stability, which is critical for building trustworthy AI systems. By quantifying whether models rely on consistent attribution patterns for similar inputs, the proposed approach supports more robust evaluation of model behavior in practical pattern recognition pipelines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/anmspro/ESS-XAI-Stability.

</details>


### [121] [The Topology of Multimodal Fusion: Why Current Architectures Fail at Creative Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04465)
*Xiujiang Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文识别出现有多模态AI架构中的拓扑结构限制，提出基于中国工艺认识论的"象"概念，建立十字框架，并通过纤维丛数学形式化，设计实验验证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态AI架构存在拓扑结构限制而非参数限制，模态可分离性假设阻碍了真正的跨模态融合。需要从哲学、认知科学和数学三个维度重新思考多模态表示的基础。

Method: 1. 哲学支柱：基于维特根斯坦说/显区分，引入中国工艺认识论的"象"概念，建立道/器×说/显十字框架；2. 认知科学支柱：通过DMN/ECN/SN三系统共激活分析，建立耦合强度×调节能力的2D参数空间；3. 数学支柱：使用纤维丛和杨-米尔斯曲率形式化；4. 提出UOO实现、ANALOGY-MM基准和META-TOP三层基准测试。

Result: 提出完整的理论框架和实验方案，包括：1. 识别接触拓扑作为模态可分离性的几何先验；2. 建立十字框架描述说/显互渗的"象"状态；3. 形式化双层级动力学（创化与化裁）；4. 设计包含明确终止标准的阶段性实验路线图。

Conclusion: 当前多模态AI的模态可分离性假设限制了其表达能力，需要拓扑结构革新而非参数优化。基于中国工艺认识论的"象"概念提供了新的理论框架，通过纤维丛数学形式化和系统性实验验证，有望突破现有架构局限。

Abstract: This paper identifies a structural limitation in current multimodal AI architectures that is topological rather than parametric. Contrastive alignment (CLIP), cross-attention fusion (GPT-4V/Gemini), and diffusion-based generation share a common geometric prior -- modal separability -- which we term contact topology. The argument rests on three pillars with philosophy as the generative center. The philosophical pillar reinterprets Wittgenstein's saying/showing distinction as a problem rather than a conclusion: where Wittgenstein chose silence, the Chinese craft epistemology tradition responded with xiang (operative schema) -- the third state emerging when saying and showing interpenetrate. A cruciform framework (dao/qi x saying/showing) positions xiang at the intersection, executing dual huacai (transformation-and-cutting) along both axes. This generates a dual-layer dynamics: chuanghua (creative transformation as spontaneous event) and huacai (its institutionalization into repeatable form). The cognitive science pillar reinterprets DMN/ECN/SN tripartite co-activation through the pathological mirror: overlap isomorphism vs. superimposition collapse in a 2D parameter space (coupling intensity x regulatory capacity). The mathematical pillar formalizes these via fiber bundles and Yang-Mills curvature, with the cruciform structure mapped to fiber bundle language. We propose UOO implementation via Neural ODEs with topological regularization, the ANALOGY-MM benchmark with error-type-ratio metric, and the META-TOP three-tier benchmark testing cross-civilizational topological isomorphism across seven archetypes. A phased experimental roadmap with explicit termination criteria ensures clean exit if falsified.

</details>


### [122] [What Makes a Sale? Rethinking End-to-End Seller--Buyer Retail Dynamics with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04468)
*Jeonghwan Choi,Jibin Hwang,Gyeonghun Sun,Minjeong Ban,Taewon Yun,Hyeonjae Cheon,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RetailSim是一个端到端零售模拟框架，统一建模从卖家说服到买家决策的多阶段零售流程，通过双协议评估验证其行为保真度和经济规律再现能力，并展示在策略评估等实际应用中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零售模拟器仅捕捉零售流程的部分方面，缺乏跨阶段依赖建模，难以评估早期决策对下游结果的影响，因此需要开发能够统一建模完整零售管道的端到端模拟框架。

Method: 提出RetailSim框架，通过多样化产品空间、角色驱动的智能体和多轮交互实现模拟保真度；采用双协议评估：人类评估行为保真度，以及针对现实经济规律的元评估。

Result: RetailSim成功再现了关键经济模式，包括人口统计购买行为、价格-需求关系、异质性价格弹性；在角色推断、买卖双方交互分析和销售策略评估等决策导向用例中展示了实际效用。

Conclusion: RetailSim作为探索零售策略的受控测试平台具有潜力，能够统一建模多阶段零售流程并验证其模拟保真度，为零售策略评估提供了有效的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Evaluating retail strategies before deployment is difficult, as outcomes are determined across multiple stages, from seller-side persuasion through buyer-seller interaction to purchase decisions. However, existing retail simulators capture only partial aspects of this process and do not model cross-stage dependencies, making it difficult to assess how early decisions affect downstream outcomes. We present RetailSim, an end-to-end retail simulation framework that models this pipeline in a unified environment, explicitly designed for simulation fidelity through diverse product spaces, persona-driven agents, and multi-turn interactions. We evaluate RetailSim with a dual protocol comprising human evaluation of behavioral fidelity and meta-evaluation against real-world economic regularities, showing that it successfully reproduces key patterns such as demographic purchasing behavior, the price-demand relationship, and heterogeneous price elasticity. We further demonstrate its practical utility via decision-oriented use cases, including persona inference, seller-buyer interaction analysis, and sales strategy evaluation, showing RetailSim's potential as a controlled testbed for exploring retail strategies.

</details>


### [123] [Same World, Differently Given: History-Dependent Perceptual Reorganization in Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04637)
*Hongju Pae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种最小架构，使智能体能够维持历史敏感的世界视角，通过慢速视角潜在变量g反馈到感知系统，实现相同观察因累积立场而不同编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索何种内部组织能让人工智能体不仅适应行为，还能维持对其世界的历史敏感视角，解决智能体如何基于累积经验形成持续视角的问题。

Method: 设计最小架构：慢速视角潜在变量g反馈到感知过程，同时通过感知处理更新g；在具有固定空间支架和感觉扰动的网格世界中进行评估；比较自适应自调制、刚性更新和始终开放更新三种机制。

Result: 1.扰动历史在名义条件恢复后仍在自适应可塑性中留下可测量残留；2.视角潜在变量重组感知编码，使相同观察因先前经验而不同表示；3.仅自适应自调制产生特征性的增长-稳定动态，不同于刚性或始终开放更新机制；总体行为保持稳定，表明主要重组发生在感知层面而非行为层面。

Conclusion: 该研究识别了人工智能体中历史依赖视角组织的最小机制，展示了通过慢速视角变量与感知系统的双向交互，智能体能够基于累积经验维持持续的世界视角。

Abstract: What kind of internal organization would allow an artificial agent not only to adapt its behavior, but to sustain a history-sensitive perspective on its world? I present a minimal architecture in which a slow perspective latent $g$ feeds back into perception and is itself updated through perceptual processing. This allows identical observations to be encoded differently depending on the agent's accumulated stance. The model is evaluated in a minimal gridworld with a fixed spatial scaffold and sensory perturbations. Across analyses, three results emerge: first, perturbation history leaves measurable residue in adaptive plasticity after nominal conditions are restored. Second, the perspective latent reorganizes perceptual encoding, such that identical observations are represented differently depending on prior experience. Third, only adaptive self-modulation yields the characteristic growth-then-stabilization dynamic, unlike rigid or always-open update regimes. Gross behavior remains stable throughout, suggesting that the dominant reorganization is perceptual rather than behavioral. Together, these findings identify a minimal mechanism for history-dependent perspectival organization in artificial agents.

</details>


### [124] [On the "Causality" Step in Policy Gradient Derivations: A Pedagogical Reconciliation of Full Return and Reward-to-Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04686)
*Nima H. Siboni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文对REINFORCE算法中"奖励累计值"替换为"未来奖励累计"的推导过程进行了严格的数学分析，澄清了传统推导中不够严谨的部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略梯度的介绍性讲解中，通常使用完整轨迹回报推导REINFORCE估计器，然后通过"因果性"说明完整回报可以被未来奖励累计替代。虽然这个说法正确，但通常的推导严谨性不足，未能清晰说明过去奖励项如何消失。本文旨在明确这一步骤，提供更严格的数学推导。

Method: 采用前缀轨迹分布和得分函数恒等式，对策略梯度估计器进行数学显式推导。通过将目标函数分解为前缀轨迹的形式，直接推导出未来奖励累计项，而不是将其作为事后无偏替换。

Result: 推导得到的估计器与传统REINFORCE估计器相同，但推导过程更加严谨。未来奖励累计项直接从目标函数的前缀轨迹分解中自然产生，而不是作为额外的启发式原则。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是概念性的：展示了未来奖励累计项如何直接从目标函数的前缀轨迹分解中产生，而不是作为完整回报的事后无偏替换。在这种表述中，传统的因果性论证成为推导的自然推论，而非额外的启发式原则。

Abstract: In introductory presentations of policy gradients, one often derives the REINFORCE estimator using the full trajectory return and then states, by ``causality,'' that the full return may be replaced by the reward-to-go. Although this statement is correct, it is frequently presented at a level of rigor that leaves unclear where the past-reward terms disappear. This short paper isolates that step and gives a mathematically explicit derivation based on prefix trajectory distributions and the score-function identity. The resulting account does not change the estimator. Its contribution is conceptual: instead of presenting reward-to-go as a post hoc unbiased replacement for full return, it shows that reward-to-go arises directly once the objective is decomposed over prefix trajectories. In this formulation, the usual causality argument is recovered as a corollary of the derivation rather than as an additional heuristic principle.

</details>


### [125] [AI Assistance Reduces Persistence and Hurts Independent Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04721)
*Grace Liu,Brian Christian,Tsvetomira Dumbalska,Michiel A. Bakker,Rachit Dubey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI助手虽然短期提升任务表现，但会降低用户坚持性和无协助时的表现，仅10分钟交互即产生显著负面影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统被优化为提供即时完整回答，缺乏人类导师那种支持长期成长、搭建学习框架、跟踪进度的协作方式。研究旨在探究这种短期导向AI协作的后果

Method: 通过随机对照试验（N=1,222）研究人机交互，在数学推理和阅读理解等多种任务中，测量AI协助对用户坚持性和无协助表现的影响

Result: AI协助短期提升表现，但显著降低用户坚持性（更容易放弃）和无AI协助时的表现。这些负面效应在仅约10分钟的AI交互后即出现

Conclusion: AI模型开发需要优先考虑搭建长期能力框架，而不仅仅是即时任务完成。坚持性是技能习得的基础，也是长期学习的最强预测因素之一

Abstract: People often optimize for long-term goals in collaboration: A mentor or companion doesn't just answer questions, but also scaffolds learning, tracks progress, and prioritizes the other person's growth over immediate results. In contrast, current AI systems are fundamentally short-sighted collaborators - optimized for providing instant and complete responses, without ever saying no (unless for safety reasons). What are the consequences of this dynamic? Here, through a series of randomized controlled trials on human-AI interactions (N = 1,222), we provide causal evidence for two key consequences of AI assistance: reduced persistence and impairment of unassisted performance. Across a variety of tasks, including mathematical reasoning and reading comprehension, we find that although AI assistance improves performance in the short-term, people perform significantly worse without AI and are more likely to give up. Notably, these effects emerge after only brief interactions with AI (approximately 10 minutes). These findings are particularly concerning because persistence is foundational to skill acquisition and is one of the strongest predictors of long-term learning. We posit that persistence is reduced because AI conditions people to expect immediate answers, thereby denying them the experience of working through challenges on their own. These results suggest the need for AI model development to prioritize scaffolding long-term competence alongside immediate task completion.

</details>


### [126] [Incompleteness of AI Safety Verification via Kolmogorov Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04876)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文证明AI安全验证存在根本性信息理论限制：任何有限的形式验证器都无法认证所有高复杂度的策略合规实例


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在安全关键领域需要满足形式化安全策略约束，但现有验证方法存在局限性。论文旨在探究这些限制是否源于内在的信息理论边界，而非仅仅是计算复杂性或模型表达能力的限制。

Method: 将策略合规性形式化为编码系统行为的验证问题，使用柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度进行分析。证明了一个不完备性结果：对于任何固定的可计算枚举的可靠验证器，存在一个阈值，超过该阈值后，复杂度超过该阈值的真实策略合规实例无法被认证。

Result: 证明了有限形式验证器无法认证所有任意高复杂度的策略合规实例，揭示了AI安全验证的根本性信息理论限制，这一限制独立于计算资源。

Conclusion: AI安全验证存在内在的信息理论限制，这促使需要采用携带证明的方法来提供实例级别的正确性保证，而非依赖通用的形式验证器。

Abstract: Ensuring that artificial intelligence (AI) systems satisfy formal safety and policy constraints is a central challenge in safety-critical domains. While limitations of verification are often attributed to combinatorial complexity and model expressiveness, we show that they arise from intrinsic information-theoretic limits. We formalize policy compliance as a verification problem over encoded system behaviors and analyze it using Kolmogorov complexity. We prove an incompleteness result: for any fixed sound computably enumerable verifier, there exists a threshold beyond which true policy-compliant instances cannot be certified once their complexity exceeds that threshold. Consequently, no finite formal verifier can certify all policy-compliant instances of arbitrarily high complexity. This reveals a fundamental limitation of AI safety verification independent of computational resources, and motivates proof-carrying approaches that provide instance-level correctness guarantees.

</details>


### [127] [Learning, Potential, and Retention: An Approach for Evaluating Adaptive AI-Enabled Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04878)
*Alexis Burgon,Berkman Sahiner,Nicholas A Petrick,Gene Pennello,Ravi K Samala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出评估医疗设备自适应AI模型的新方法，包含学习、潜力和保留三个互补测量指标，用于区分模型适应与动态环境变化导致的性能变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备中自适应AI模型的评估面临挑战，因为模型和评估数据集的迭代更新使性能评估复杂化。需要区分性能变化是由模型适应引起还是由动态环境变化导致

Method: 引入包含三个互补测量的新方法：学习（模型在当前数据上的改进）、潜力（数据集驱动的性能变化）、保留（跨修改步骤的知识保存），以解耦模型适应与动态环境变化对性能的影响

Result: 通过模拟人口变化的案例研究表明：渐进过渡可实现稳定的学习和保留，而快速变化则揭示可塑性与稳定性之间的权衡。这些测量为监管科学提供了实用见解

Conclusion: 该方法能够对自适应AI系统在连续修改过程中的安全性和有效性进行严格评估，为医疗设备自适应AI模型的监管评估提供了实用框架

Abstract: This work addresses challenges in evaluating adaptive artificial intelligence (AI) models for medical devices, where iterative updates to both models and evaluation datasets complicate performance assessment. We introduce a novel approach with three complementary measurements: learning (model improvement on current data), potential (dataset-driven performance shifts), and retention (knowledge preservation across modification steps), to disentangle performance changes caused by model adaptations versus dynamic environments. Case studies using simulated population shifts demonstrate the approach's utility: gradual transitions enable stable learning and retention, while rapid shifts reveal trade-offs between plasticity and stability. These measurements provide practical insights for regulatory science, enabling rigorous assessment of the safety and effectiveness of adaptive AI systems over sequential modifications.

</details>


### [128] [QED-Nano: Teaching a Tiny Model to Prove Hard Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04898)
*LM-Provers,Yuxiao Qu,Amrith Setlur,Jasper Dekoninck,Edward Beeching,Jia Li,Ian Wu,Lewis Tunstall,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 4B参数开源模型QED-Nano通过三阶段训练方法在奥林匹克数学证明问题上达到接近专有模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有AI系统在复杂证明问题上表现出色但训练流程不透明、依赖大型内部模型和框架，导致运行昂贵、难以复现和研究。研究小型开源模型是否也能在奥林匹克数学推理上达到竞争性性能。

Method: 三阶段训练方法：1) 监督微调从DeepSeek-Math-V2蒸馏良好证明写作风格；2) 基于评分标准的强化学习；3) 扩展强化学习，使用推理缓存将长证明分解为迭代的总结-精炼循环。

Result: QED-Nano在证明生成性能上超越了许多更大的开源模型（包括Nomos-1和GPT-OSS-120B），接近Gemini 3 Pro等专有模型性能，且推理成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: 小型开源模型通过精心设计的训练方法可以在奥林匹克数学推理上达到竞争性性能，为开放数学推理研究提供了完整可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Proprietary AI systems have recently demonstrated impressive capabilities on complex proof-based problems, with gold-level performance reported at the 2025 International Mathematical Olympiad (IMO). However, the training pipelines behind these systems remain largely undisclosed, and their reliance on large "internal" models and scaffolds makes them expensive to run, difficult to reproduce, and hard to study or improve upon. This raises a central question: can small, open models also be trained to achieve competitive reasoning performance on difficult Olympiad-level math? In this paper, we answer this question by building QED-Nano, a 4B model post-trained for Olympiad-level proofs. Our training recipe has three stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning to imbue good proof-writing styles by distilling from DeepSeek-Math-V2, (2) reinforcement learning (RL) with rubric-based rewards, and (3) expanding RL with a reasoning cache, which decomposes long proofs into iterative summarize-and-refine cycles and enables stronger test-time reasoning. QED-Nano surpasses the proof-generation performance of much larger open models, including Nomos-1 and GPT-OSS-120B, and approaches the performance of proprietary models like Gemini 3 Pro, at a fraction of the inference cost. To support further research on open mathematical reasoning, we release the full QED-Nano pipeline, including the QED-Nano and QED-Nano-SFT models, the FineProofs-SFT and FineProofs-RL datasets, and the training and evaluation code.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Optimal Experimental Design using Eigenvalue-Based Criteria with Pyomo.DoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03354)
*Daniel J. Laky,Shammah Lilonfe,Shawn B. Martin,Katherine A. Klise,Bethany L. Nicholson,John D. Siirola,Alexander W. Dowling*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: Pyomo.DoE软件包扩展了基于特征值的实验设计指标计算能力，支持方程导向优化框架中的E-最优性和ME-最优性设计，并引入了新的实验创建建模抽象，简化了数字孪生工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生需要高质量数据来实现预测能力，但时间和资源限制使得高效实验设计至关重要。基于模型的实验设计可以解决这一挑战，特别是在结合方程导向优化和第一性原理模型时。然而，在Pyomo.DoE中嵌入线性代数操作（如矩阵求逆和特征值计算）仍然困难。

Method: 扩展Pyomo.DoE软件包，添加基于回调的能力，支持在方程导向优化框架中严格计算基于特征值的设计指标，包括最小特征值最优性（E-最优性）和条件数最优性（ME-最优性）。同时提出新的实验创建建模抽象，用于Pyomo中的侵入式不确定性量化，减少用户建模工作量。

Result: 实现了在Pyomo.DoE中计算E-最优性和ME-最优性设计指标的能力，使实验设计能够直接关注信息不足或数值问题参数方向。新的建模抽象使模型和软件抽象在数字孪生工作流程中对齐，减少了用户建模工作量。

Conclusion: 这项工作扩展了Pyomo.DoE中可用的实用最优设计标准范围，改进了构建和优化高价值数字孪生的工作流程，为基于方程导向模型的实验设计提供了更强大的工具支持。

Abstract: Digital twins require high-quality data to achieve predictive capability, but time and resource limitations make efficient experiment design essential. Model-based design of experiments can address this challenge, especially when coupled with equation-oriented optimization and first-principles models. Pyomo.DoE is a software package for optimal experimental design of high-fidelity, equation-oriented models; however, embedding linear algebra operations such as matrix inversion and eigenvalue computation within these optimization problems remains difficult. This work extends Pyomo.DoE with callback-based capabilities that enable rigorous computation of eigenvalue-based design metrics, including minimum eigenvalue optimality (E-optimality) and condition number optimality (ME-optimality), within equation-oriented optimization frameworks. These additions allow experimental design to focus directly on poorly informed or numerically problematic parameter directions. We also present a new experiment-creation modeling abstraction for intrusive uncertainty quantification in Pyomo that reduces user modeling effort by aligning model and software abstractions across the digital twin workflow. In addition, a brief tutorial on experimental design metrics is provided in the methodology and supplementary information. Overall, this work expands the range of practical optimal design criteria available in Pyomo.DoE and improves the workflow for building and refining high-value digital twins.

</details>


### [130] [Impulse-to-Peak-Output Norm Optimal State-Feedback Control of Linear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03399)
*Tristan Thomas,Sachin Shivakumar,Javad Mohammadpour Velni*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将ODE系统的脉冲到峰值响应分析扩展到PDE系统，利用PIE表示和Lyapunov理论，通过凸优化求解PDE的I2P范数，并开发了I2P最优状态反馈控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ODE系统的I2P最优控制技术无法直接扩展到PDE系统，因为PDE缺乏通用的传递函数和状态空间表示。需要开发适用于PDE的I2P分析和控制方法。

Method: 采用部分积分方程表示作为PDE的状态空间表示，利用Lyapunov稳定性理论将I2P响应分析问题表述为可解的凸优化问题。通过建立优化问题的强对偶性，开发了I2P最优状态反馈控制方法。

Result: 获得了线性PDE系统I2P范数的可证明边界，建立了I2P最优控制的构造性方法，并在多个示例中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将ODE的I2P分析技术扩展到PDE系统，通过PIE框架和Lyapunov理论解决了PDE的I2P最优控制问题，为PDE控制提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Impulse-to-peak response (I2P) analysis for state-space ordinary differential equation (ODE) systems is a well-studied classical problem. However, the techniques employed for I2P optimal control of ODEs have not been extended to partial differential equation (PDE) systems due to the lack of a universal transfer function and state-space representation. Recently, however, partial integral equation (PIE) representation was proposed as the desired state-space representation of a PDE, and Lyapunov stability theory was used to solve various control problems, such as stability and optimal ${H}_\infty$ control. In this work, we utilize this PIE framework, and associated Lyapunov techniques, to formulate the I2P response analysis problem as a solvable convex optimization and obtain provable bounds for the I2P-norm of linear PDEs. Moreover, by establishing strong duality between primal and dual formulations of the optimization problem, we develop a constructive method for I2P optimal state-feedback control of PDEs and demonstrate the effectiveness of the method on various examples.

</details>


### [131] [Reach-Avoid Model Predictive Control with Guaranteed Recursive Feasibility via Input Constrained Backstepping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03407)
*Jianqiang Ding,Nishant Jayesh Bhave,Shankar A. Deka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于连续仿射非线性系统的采样数据模型预测控制框架，提供严格的到达-避免和递归可行性保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对连续仿射非线性系统，在物理约束下提供严格的到达-避免和递归可行性保证是控制领域的重要挑战

Method: 通过反步法传播输入和输出约束，构建满足控制输入限制的到达-避免不变集，并将其作为终端集，在快速采样条件下证明采样数据MPC的递归可行性

Result: 提出的采样数据MPC框架能够递归地提供可行控制输入，安全地将连续系统引导至目标集，数值结果验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该框架为连续仿射非线性系统提供了一种具有严格理论保证的采样数据MPC方法，在物理约束下实现了到达-避免和递归可行性

Abstract: This letter proposes a novel sampled-data model predictive control framework for continuous control-affine nonlinear systems that provides rigorous reach-avoid and recursive feasibility guarantees under physical constraints. By propagating both input and output constraints through backstepping process, we present a constructive approach to synthesize a reach-avoid invariant set that complies with control input limits. Using this reach-avoid set as a terminal set, we prove that the proposed sampled-data MPC framework recursively admits feasible control inputs that safely steer the continuous system into the target set under fast sampling conditions. Numerical results demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [132] [High-Order Matrix Control Barrier Functions: Well-Posedness and Feasibility via Matrix Relative Degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03450)
*Samuel G. Gessow,Pio Ong,Aaron D. Ames,Brett T. Lopez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了高阶矩阵控制屏障函数（HOMCBFs），用于在控制输入不直接出现在一阶导数的高阶动力学系统中强制执行矩阵值安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多安全约束更自然地表示为矩阵值条件（如正定性或特征值边界），但现有标量CBF方法会引入非光滑性。矩阵CBFs（MCBFs）虽然解决了这一问题，但对于控制输入不直接出现在一阶导数的情况，需要高阶扩展，而这在矩阵情况下尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 开发了高阶矩阵控制屏障函数（HOMCBFs），建立了确保相关约束适定性和可行性的条件。特别提出了最优衰减HOMCBF公式，使得仅需在最小特征子空间上进行控制即可确保前向不变性。

Result: HOMCBFs框架能够为具有高阶动力学的系统强制执行矩阵值安全约束。通过最优衰减HOMCBF公式，可以在仅控制最小特征子空间的情况下确保前向不变性。该框架在具有非线性测量模型的双积分器系统的定位安全问题上得到验证，通过强制执行信息矩阵的正定性来展示其有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功扩展了矩阵控制屏障函数到高阶情况，为处理矩阵值安全约束的高阶动力学系统提供了理论框架和实用方法，填补了现有研究的空白。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide an effective framework for enforcing safety in dynamical systems with scalar constraints. However, many safety constraints are more naturally expressed as matrix-valued conditions, such as positive definiteness or eigenvalue bounds - scalar formulations introduce potential nonsmoothness that complicates analysis. Matrix control barrier functions (MCBFs) address this limitation by directly enforcing matrix-valued safety constraints. Yet for constraints where the control input does not appear in the first derivative, high-order formulations are required. While such extensions are well understood in the scalar case, they remain largely unexplored in the matrix case. This paper develops high-order matrix control barrier functions (HOMCBFs) and establishes conditions ensuring well-posedness and feasibility of the associated constraints, enabling enforcement of matrix-valued safety constraints for systems with high-order dynamics. We further show that, using an optimal-decay HOMCBF formulation, forward invariance can be ensured while requiring control only over the minimum eigenspace. The framework is demonstrated on a localization safety problem by enforcing positive definiteness of the information matrix for a double integrator system with a nonlinear measurement model.

</details>


### [133] [SDP Approach to Quadratic Vertex-Disjoint Paths Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03452)
*Mingming Xu,Hao Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解二次k顶点不相交路径问题的分支定界算法，通过图约简、SDP松弛和ADMM求解，在计算性能上优于Gurobi。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二次k顶点不相交路径问题（Q-k-VDP），该问题在定向图中寻找k条顶点不相交路径以最小化非凸二次目标函数。该问题具有挑战性，需要高效算法处理大规模实例。

Method: 1. 将问题建模为二元二次规划；2. 应用系统图约简管理维度；3. 去除子回路消除约束，推导半定规划松弛；4. 在分支定界框架中求解松弛模型；5. 使用定制化的交替方向乘子法计算SDP松弛的界。

Result: 计算结果表明，所提方法在求解更多实例到最优解方面持续优于Gurobi，特别是在具有挑战性的大规模实例上表现突出。

Conclusion: 提出的基于SDP松弛和ADMM的分支定界算法能有效求解Q-k-VDP问题，为处理非凸二次组合优化问题提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the quadratic $k$-vertex-disjoint paths problem (Q-$k$-VDP), which seeks $k$ vertex-disjoint paths in a directed graph that minimize a nonconvex quadratic objective function. We formulate the problem as a binary quadratic program and apply a systematic graph reduction to manage its dimensionality. To obtain a tractable bounding model, we drop the subtour-elimination constraints and derive a semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation. We then solve this relaxed model within a branch-and-bound framework, where the bounds are computed from the SDP relaxation using a tailored alternating direction method of multipliers. Computational results show that our proposed method consistently outperforms Gurobi by solving more instances to optimality, especially on challenging large-scale instances.

</details>


### [134] [Scheduling Electricity Production Units to Mitigate Severe Weather Impact: An Efficient Computational Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03475)
*Yongzheng Dai,Antonio J. Conejo,Feng Qiu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段鲁棒优化框架，通过预先安排发电机组（特别是慢启动机组）来应对极端天气导致的输电线路故障，以最小化最坏情况下的负荷削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气事件可能导致输电线路跳闸或物理损坏，引发大规模负荷削减。现有研究在物理定律描述和计算效率方面存在不足，需要更精确的模型和高效算法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段鲁棒优化模型：第一阶段安排发电机组调度，第二阶段在最坏情况下进行电力调度以最小化负荷削减。结合凸化的交流潮流约束确保系统可靠性，并开发了基于列约束生成框架的问题特定外逼近算法。

Result: 计算结果表明，所提出的模型和算法能够在合理时间内为中等规模实例提供标准最优性间隙内的解决方案，相比现有求解器具有更高的计算效率。

Conclusion: 该框架通过更精确的物理定律描述和高效求解算法，有效解决了极端天气下电力系统负荷削减问题，为电力系统运行提供了实用的决策支持工具。

Abstract: In the electric system, extreme weather events can cause trips or physical damage to transmission lines, leading to large-scale load shedding. To mitigate power shedding, we propose a framework that pre-positions the commitment of production units--particularly slow-start units--to cope with transmission topologies that may result from such events. Our goal is to minimize load shedding under the worst-case scenario. The novel contributions of this paper are twofold: (1) a more precise description of the physical laws than those used in previous works reported in the literature, and (2) a highly efficient solution algorithm compared to state-of-the-art, off-the-shelf solvers. We formulate this framework as a two-stage robust optimization model. In the first stage, generation units are scheduled, and in the second stage, power dispatch decisions are made to minimize load shedding under the worst-case scenario. Convexified AC power flow constraints are incorporated to ensure system reliability and security. The resulting formulation is a tri-level mixed-integer nonlinear optimization problem. To address the computational challenges, we propose a problem-specific outer approximation algorithm embedded within a column-and-constraint generation framework. Computational results demonstrate that our model and algorithm can produce solutions within a standard optimality gap in a reasonable time for moderately large instances.

</details>


### [135] [A Hybrid Algorithm for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03658)
*Reza Rahimi Baghbadorani,Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani,Sergio Grammatico*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 基于自适应黄金比例算法(aGRAAL)提出两种新的单调变分不等式求解方法，通过选择超越黄金比例的动量参数提升收敛速度，并研究大小动量参数切换以加速收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受自适应黄金比例算法(aGRAAL)启发，发现选择超越黄金比例的动量参数可以改进收敛速度，这促使研究在大小动量参数之间切换以进一步加速收敛

Method: 提出两种新方法：1）基于aGRAAL但选择超越黄金比例的动量参数；2）研究在大小动量参数之间切换的策略以加速收敛。在多种变分不等式问题上验证性能

Result: 在机器学习和控制文献中的多个变分不等式问题上验证了算法性能，包括纳什均衡寻求、复合最小化、马尔可夫决策过程和零和博弈，并与现有方法进行比较

Conclusion: 通过超越黄金比例的动量参数选择和动量参数切换策略，能够有效加速单调变分不等式的求解收敛速度，在多种实际问题上表现出优于现有方法的性能

Abstract: Inspired by the adaptive Golden Ratio Algorithm (aGRAAL), we propose two new methods for solving monotone variational inequalities. We show that by selecting the momentum parameter beyond the golden ratio in aGRAAL, the convergence speed can be improved, which motivates us to study the switching between small and large momentum parameters to accelerate convergence. We validate the performance of our proposed algorithms on several classes of variational inequality problems studied in the machine learning and control literature, including Nash equilibrium seeking, composite minimization, Markov decision processes, and zero-sum games, and compare them to that of existing methods.

</details>


### [136] [Robust self-testing with CHSH mod 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03700)
*Igor Klep,Nando Leijenhorst,Victor Magron*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 解决了CHSH mod 3 Bell不等式的最大量子违反值和自测试性质，确定了精确的最大量子值，证明了存在唯一的（模对称性）最优不可约策略，并建立了近似最优策略的鲁棒性界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHSH mod 3 Bell不等式作为高维量子非定域性的自然测试平台，其最大量子违反值和自测试性质一直未得到解决。该研究旨在填补这一空白，为高维量子非定域性提供严格的理论基础。

Method: 通过理论分析确定CHSH mod 3 Bell不等式的精确最大量子值。证明在酉等价和不等式自然对称性下，存在唯一的最优不可约策略（等价于四个对称相关的最优不可约策略）。进一步证明任何接近最优值的策略，在局部等距变换下，都近似于最优不可约策略的直和。

Result: 确定了CHSH mod 3 Bell不等式的精确最大量子违反值。证明了最优策略使用最大纠缠的两qutrit态，且存在唯一的（模对称性）最优不可约策略。建立了鲁棒性结果：任何与最优值相差ε的策略，在局部等距变换下，都与最优不可约策略的直和相差O(√ε)。

Conclusion: 该研究完全解决了CHSH mod 3 Bell不等式的最大量子违反和自测试问题，为高维量子非定域性提供了严格的理论框架，并建立了鲁棒性界限，对量子信息处理和量子基础研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: The CHSH mod 3 Bell inequality is a natural testbed for higher-dimensional quantum nonlocality, yet its maximal quantum violation and self-testing properties have remained unresolved. We determine its exact maximal quantum value and show that, up to unitary equivalence and the natural symmetries of the inequality, it admits a unique optimal irreducible strategy; equivalently, there are four symmetry-related optimal irreducible strategies. Each of these strategies uses a maximally entangled two-qutrit state. We further prove that any strategy whose value is within $\varepsilon$ of the optimum is $O(\sqrt{\varepsilon})$-close, up to local isometries, to a direct sum of optimal irreducible strategies.

</details>


### [137] [Carbon-Driven Hierarchical Incentive Mechanism for Renewable Power-to-Ammonia Production in Carbon and Ammonia Transactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03728)
*Yangjun Zeng,Huayan Geng,Yiwei Qiu,Xiuli Sun,Liuchao Xu,Jiarong Li,Shi Chen,Buxiang Zhou,Kaigui Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种分层碳驱动激励机制（PCIM），通过碳配额和氨市场的双层博弈框架，提升绿色氨的市场竞争力，并设计了激励相容的碳配额分配机制（PCAM）来确保可再生电制氨产业链的利益公平分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生电制氨（ReP2A）作为电力与化工部门脱碳的重要途径，缺乏能够连接ReP2A生产商与化石基灰氨（GA）竞争者、同时协调ReP2A产业链中可再生能源、绿氢和绿氨生产商利益的碳相关机制。

Method: 构建双层博弈框架：外层为ReP2A和GA在碳配额市场和氨市场的交易；内层为ReP2A产业链中的电力和氢交易。采用可分解等价优化处理内层，基于KKT条件将外层转化为混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解。基于均衡结果提出激励相容的碳配额分配机制（PCAM）。

Result: PCIM能够减少12.9%的碳排放，而行业总收入仅下降1.8%。PCIM结果为碳定价提供指导，PCAM的应用提高了利益相关方参与ReP2A生产的意愿。

Conclusion: 提出的分层碳驱动激励机制有效提升了绿色氨的市场竞争力，通过合理的碳配额分配机制确保了产业链利益公平分配，为可再生电制氨的规模化发展提供了可行的政策工具。

Abstract: Renewable power-to-ammonia (ReP2A) production offers a viable pathway to decarbonize the power and chemical sectors and is increasingly supported by carbon-emission policies. However, a carbon-related mechanism that links ReP2A producers with fossil-based gray ammonia (GA) competitors while aligning the interests of renewable power, green hydrogen, and green ammonia producers in the ReP2A process chain remains unexplored. To fill this gap, we propose a hierarchical carbon-driven incentive mechanism (PCIM) to improve the market competitiveness of green ammonia. We first construct a trading framework in which ReP2A and GA participate in both the carbon allowance (CA) and ammonia markets, which forms the outer layer. These interactions, together with electricity and hydrogen transactions in the ReP2A chain, which form the inner layer, are modeled as a hierarchical game. For tractability, the inner layer is characterized via decomposable equivalent optimization, and the outer layer is solved as a mixed-integer linear program (MILP) derived from Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions. Based on the resulting equilibrium, we identify the carbon-related revenue of ReP2A and propose an incentive-compatible CA allocation mechanism (PCAM) %to ensure equitable benefit sharing across the ReP2A chain. Simulations show that the PCIM reduces carbon emissions by 12.9\% at a cost of only a 1.8% decrease in sectorwide revenue, and results from the PCIM provide guidance for carbon pricing. Furthermore, the application of the PCAM increases stakeholders' willingness to participate in ReP2A production.

</details>


### [138] [Ordinary differential equations for regularized variational problems involving semi-discrete optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03780)
*Adrien Cances,Luca Nenna,Daniyar Omarov,Brendan Pass*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究熵正则化半离散变分问题，建立解在正则化参数下的ODE刻画，提供数值求解的稳健方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究涉及最优传输和其他项的变分问题，传统数值方法如牛顿法需要良好初始化且可能不稳定，需要更稳健的求解策略

Method: 证明解可由正则化参数下的适定常微分方程刻画，初始条件对应完全正则化问题的显式解，通过求解ODE得到任意正则化程度的解

Result: 建立解在正则化参数下的连续性，确保极限得到完全非正则化问题的解；对非最优传输项不随正则化参数缩放的问题版本也建立类似结果

Conclusion: ODE刻画提供数值求解的稳健方法，优于牛顿法等传统方法，无需任意初始化，通过标准ODE方法可有效求解示例问题

Abstract: We consider entropically regularized, semi-discrete versions of variational problems on the set of probability measures involving optimal transport as well as other terms. We prove that the solutions can be characterized by well-posed ordinary differential equations in the regularization parameter. The initial conditions for these equations, corresponding to solutions to completely regularized problems, are typically known explicitly. The ODE can then be solved to recover the solution for an arbitrary degree of regularization; we verify that the solution is continuous in the regularization parameter, implying that taking the limit of the trajectory yields the solution to the fully unregularized problem. We establish analogous results for a version of the problem when the non-optimal transport term is not scaled with the regularization parameter. We exploit our characterization to numerically solve several example problems using standard ODE methods; this strategy exhibits superior robustness to alternatives such as Newton's method, as arbitrary initializations are not required.

</details>


### [139] [An Improved Last-Iterate Convergence Rate for Anchored Gradient Descent Ascent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03782)
*Anja Surina,Arun Suggala,George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 证明了Anchored Gradient Descent Ascent算法在光滑凸凹min-max问题中具有精确的$\mathcal{O}(1/t)$最后迭代收敛率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决之前工作中存在的开放性问题：之前的研究建立了$\mathcal{O}(1/t^{2-2p})$的收敛率（$p \in (1/2, 1)$），但无法确定是否能够达到改进的精确$\mathcal{O}(1/t)$收敛率

Method: 分析Anchored Gradient Descent Ascent算法在光滑凸凹min-max问题中的收敛性，使用AI系统自动生成Lean形式化证明

Result: 成功证明了Anchored Gradient Descent Ascent算法确实可以达到精确的$\mathcal{O}(1/t)$最后迭代收敛率，解决了之前的开放性问题

Conclusion: 该研究通过AI辅助的形式化证明方法，确认了Anchored Gradient Descent Ascent算法在光滑凸凹min-max问题中具有最优的$\mathcal{O}(1/t)$最后迭代收敛率

Abstract: We analyze the last-iterate convergence of the Anchored Gradient Descent Ascent algorithm for smooth convex-concave min-max problems. While previous work established a last-iterate rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/t^{2-2p})$ for the squared gradient norm, where $p \in (1/2, 1)$, it remained an open problem whether the improved exact $\mathcal{O}(1/t)$ rate is achievable. In this work, we resolve this question in the affirmative. This result was discovered autonomously by an AI system capable of writing formal proofs in Lean. The Lean proof can be accessed at https://github.com/google-deepmind/formal-conjectures/pull/3675/commits/a13226b49fd3b897f4c409194f3bcbeb96a08515

</details>


### [140] [Acceleration of Moment Bound Optimization for Stochastic Chemical Reactions Using Reaction-wise Sparsity of Moment Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03791)
*Tomoki Sadatoshi,Antonis Papachristodoulou,Yutaka Hori*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种利用稀疏性的矩阵分解方法，用于降低随机化学动力学中矩边界半定规划的计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化学动力学中的矩动力学通常涉及无限链式耦合方程，低阶矩依赖于高阶矩，导致解析求解困难。现有的矩边界半定规划方法虽然能提供有保证的上下界，但面临半定约束规模随分子物种数量组合增长的快速膨胀问题，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出一种利用稀疏性的矩阵分解方法：首先表征矩方程的稀疏结构（每个反应仅涉及由其反应物确定的变量子集），然后利用这种结构将半定约束分解为更小的约束，从而降低优化问题的计算成本。

Result: 该方法显著降低了优化问题的计算成本，同时提供了实际可用的矩边界。通过分解半定约束，有效缓解了约束规模随变量数量组合增长的问题。

Conclusion: 提出的稀疏性利用矩阵分解方法能够有效降低随机化学动力学中矩边界半定规划的计算复杂度，为解决大规模矩边界问题提供了可行的计算框架。

Abstract: Moment dynamics in stochastic chemical kinetics often involve an infinite chain of coupled equations, where lower-order moments depend on higher-order ones, making them analytically intractable. Moment bounding via semidefinite programming provides guaranteed upper and lower bounds on stationary moments. However, this formulation suffers from the rapidly growing size of semidefinite constraints due to the combinatorial growth of moments with the number of molecular species. In this paper, we propose a sparsity-exploiting matrix decomposition method for semidefinite constraints in stationary moment bounding problems to reduce the computational cost of the resulting semidefinite programs. Specifically, we characterize the sparsity structure of moment equations, where each reaction involves only a subset of variables determined by its reactants, and exploit this structure to decompose the semidefinite constraints into smaller ones. We demonstrate that the resulting formulation reduces the computational cost of the optimization problem while providing practically useful bounds.

</details>


### [141] [Computing Rare Probabilities of Voltage Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03807)
*Tongtong Jin,Anirudh Subramanyam,D. Adrian Maldonado*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 基于大偏差理论框架计算电压崩溃的罕见概率，通过寻找稳定性边界上的最可能故障点，提供一阶和二阶近似方法，二阶方法考虑边界曲率提高精度，在罕见事件区域收敛于蒙特卡洛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒙特卡洛方法在计算电压崩溃等罕见事件概率时计算成本过高，需要开发更高效准确的计算方法。大偏差理论为罕见概率计算提供了理论基础，能够处理高斯和非高斯不确定性。

Method: 基于大偏差理论框架，将电压崩溃概率计算问题转化为寻找稳定性边界上的最可能故障点（瞬时子）。推导了一阶近似（基于马氏距离）和二阶近似（考虑稳定性边界局部曲率）方法。该方法可扩展到非高斯不确定性。

Result: 在测试系统上验证表明，大偏差理论估计在罕见事件区域收敛于蒙特卡洛结果，而直接采样在计算上不可行。二阶方法因考虑边界曲率而具有更高精度。

Conclusion: 大偏差理论框架为电压崩溃罕见概率计算提供了准确高效的方法，推广了基于马氏距离的方法，可处理非高斯不确定性，在罕见事件区域具有计算优势。

Abstract: This paper introduces a framework based on Large Deviation Theory (LDT) to accurately and efficiently compute the rare probabilities of voltage collapse. We formulate the problem as finding the most probable failure point (the instanton) on the stability boundary and derive both first-order and second-order approximations for the collapse probability. The second-order method incorporates the local curvature of the stability boundary, yielding higher accuracy. This LDT framework generalizes methods based on Mahalanobis distance and is extensible to non-Gaussian uncertainties. We validate our approach on test systems, demonstrating that the LDT estimates converge to Monte Carlo results in the rare-event regime where direct sampling becomes computationally prohibitive.

</details>


### [142] [Distributed Optimal Consensus of Nonlinear Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03958)
*Ziyuan Guo,Chuanzhi lv,Liping Zhang,Huanshui Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于最优控制原理和模型预测控制的分布式最优一致性算法，用于一般非线性多智能体系统，涵盖无领导和领导-跟随两种情况


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般非线性多智能体系统的最优一致性问题，现有方法在处理一般非线性系统时存在局限性，需要开发更广泛适用的分布式最优一致性算法

Method: 1. 基于最优控制原理（OCP）的分布式最优一致性算法；2. 在模型预测控制（MPC）框架下开发两种增强算法，提高对一般非线性多智能体系统的适用性

Result: 算法收敛性和超线性收敛速率得到严格理论分析，数值仿真验证了所提算法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的分布式最优一致性算法在统一框架下解决了非线性多智能体系统的一致性问题，MPC增强算法具有更广泛的适用性，为非线性多智能体系统控制提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: In this paper, the optimal consensus problem for general nonlinear multi-agent systems is studied, where both leaderless and leader-follower cases are considered in a unified framework. The key idea is to convert consensus problems into optimal control problems where the objective of each agent with nonlinear dynamics is to design the control input minimizing the global consensus cost function. Compared with the existing distributed consensus control for nonlinear multi-agent systems, we propose a distributed optimal consensus algorithm based on the optimal control principle (OCP) method, and two enhanced algorithms are developed under the model predictive control (MPC) framework,these two algorithms demonstrate broader applicability when handling general nonlinear multi-agent systems. Moreover, the convergence and superlinear convergence rate of the proposed algorithms are rigorously analyzed. Numerical simulations demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [143] [Robust $\Hinf$ Observer Design via Finsler's Lemma and IQCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03989)
*Raktim Bhattacharya,Felix Biertümpfel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Finsler的LMI方法，用于鲁棒H∞观测器设计，结合积分二次约束和块结构不确定性，通过引入松弛变量解决标准块对角方法的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准块对角方法存在两个主要限制：1) LMI要求He{PA} < 0在边缘稳定动力学中失效；2) 乘子-李雅普诺夫权衡导致宽不确定性范围下不可行。需要开发更灵活的框架来处理这些限制

Method: 引入松弛变量来松弛李雅普诺夫矩阵、观测器增益和IQC乘子之间的耦合，形成基于Finsler的LMI框架。对于边缘稳定动力学，在设计模型中引入人工阻尼来平衡认证性能与实际性能

Result: 所提框架成功应用于两个案例：四元数姿态估计（角速度不确定性）和质量-弹簧-阻尼器状态估计（物理参数不确定性），证明了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 基于Finsler的LMI方法克服了标准块对角方法的局限性，为具有块结构不确定性的鲁棒H∞观测器设计提供了更灵活可行的框架

Abstract: This paper develops a Finsler-based LMI for robust $\Hinf$ observer design with integral quadratic constraints (IQCs) and block-structured uncertainty. By introducing a slack variable that relaxes the coupling between the Lyapunov matrix, the observer gain, and the IQC multiplier, the formulation addresses two limitations of the standard block-diagonal approach: the LMI requirement $\He{PA} \prec 0$ (which fails for marginally stable dynamics), and a multiplier--Lyapunov trade-off that causes infeasibility for wide uncertainty ranges. For marginally stable dynamics, artificial damping in the design model balances certified versus actual performance. The framework is demonstrated on quaternion attitude estimation with angular velocity uncertainty and mass-spring-damper state estimation with uncertain physical parameters.

</details>


### [144] [A Periodic Dichotomy in Linear Control Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04003)
*Shichao Ye,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 构建周期性二分变换并应用于周期性线性二次最优控制问题，得到最优极值的显式表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期性线性二次最优控制问题中如何获得最优极值的显式表示，解决传统方法中难以直接表征最优解的问题

Method: 通过周期性Riccati和Lyapunov方程的解构造周期性二分变换，利用该变换推导最优极值的显式表达式

Result: 在适当的指数可镇定和可检测假设下，建立了最优极值的完整表征，提供了周期性线性二次最优控制问题的最优极值的显式表示

Conclusion: 周期性二分变换为周期性线性二次最优控制问题提供了有效的分析工具，能够显式地表征最优极值，扩展了相关理论结果

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a periodic dichotomy transformation using solutions of periodic Riccati and Lyapunov equations. As an application of this transformation, we provide an explicit representation of the optimal extremal for periodic linear quadratic optimal control problems. Specifically, we establish a complete characterization of the optimal extremal under suitable exponential stabilizability and detectability assumptions.

</details>


### [145] [Optimal triangulations for piecewise linear approximations of non-convex variable products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04026)
*Robert Burlacu,Lukas Hager,Robert Hildebrand*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文解决了平面内不定二次函数分段线性逼近的最优三角剖分问题，证明了允许顶点变化偏差可将三角密度降低25%，达到全局最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Pottmann等人2000年提出的关于不定二次函数分段线性逼近最优三角剖分的猜想，以及Atariah等人2018年工作中留下的开放性问题，即他们的常数偏差构造是否最优。

Method: 通过允许三角顶点处存在变化偏差（而非常数偏差），构建更优的三角剖分方案，并证明其在L∞范数误差约束下的全局最优性。同时分析平行四边形平铺结构下的最优性。

Result: 1. 允许顶点变化偏差可将最优三角密度相比Atariah等人的常数偏差构造降低25%；2. 证明该构造是全局最优的；3. 对于平行四边形平铺结构，证明Atariah等人的常数偏差构造在要求逼近连续时是最优的。

Conclusion: 不定二次函数的一般分段线性逼近潜力显著低于定二次函数，因为前者只能将三角密度降低25%，而后者可降低50%。同时提出猜想：Atariah等人的常数偏差构造对所有连续三角剖分（不仅限于平行四边形结构）都是最优的。

Abstract: We show optimal triangulations for piecewise linear (PWL) approximations of indefinite quadratic functions over the plane. Optimal triangulations have minimum triangle density while allowing a PWL approximation that fulfills a prescribed error bound measured in the L-infinity norm. In 2000, Pottmann et al. proved optimal triangulations for PWL interpolations and conjectured that these are also optimal for general PWL approximations. This conjecture was refuted in 2018 by Atariah et al., who allowed a constant deviation at the vertices of the triangles and decreased the triangle density by roughly 3%, though they left open whether their construction was optimal. In this paper, we resolve this open question: allowing varying deviations at vertices reduces the optimal triangle density by 25% compared to Atariah et al., and we prove this is globally optimal. We thus show that the potential of general PWL approximations is significantly lower for indefinite than for definite quadratic functions, where the triangle density can be halved when allowing general approximations compared to interpolations. Furthermore, we prove that among parallelogram tilings -- triangulations built from translated copies of a triangle and its point-reflection -- the constant-deviation construction of Atariah et al. is optimal when continuity of the PWL approximation is required. We conjecture that this optimality extends to all continuous triangulations, not just those based on parallelograms.

</details>


### [146] [Primal-Dual Methods for Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization with Orthogonality Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04130)
*Linglingzhi Zhu,Wentao Ding,Shangyuan Liu,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种免回缩的线性化平滑增广拉格朗日方法，用于求解非光滑非凸正交约束优化问题，具有单循环、无子问题求解特点，计算效率高且可扩展性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交约束优化问题在数据科学中日益重要，黎曼方法因Stiefel流形的计算优势而流行。然而，非光滑性与正交约束的结合给现有黎曼方法带来可扩展性、并行性、复杂子问题和累积数值误差等挑战。

Method: 采用免回缩的原对偶方法，提出线性化平滑增广拉格朗日方法，专门针对非光滑非凸正交约束优化。该方法为单循环结构，无需求解子问题。

Result: 证明了算法具有O(ε^{-3})的迭代复杂度以找到ε-KKT点，与黎曼优化文献中最佳结果匹配。利用标准KL性质证明了算法的渐近序列收敛性。数值实验显示该方法在光滑和非光滑正交约束问题上均优于现有算法。

Conclusion: 提出的线性化平滑增广拉格朗日方法有效解决了非光滑正交约束优化的挑战，在计算效率、可扩展性和理论保证方面均表现优异，为相关领域提供了有力的数值求解工具。

Abstract: Recent advancements in data science have significantly elevated the importance of orthogonally constrained optimization problems. The Riemannian approach has become a popular technique for addressing these problems due to the advantageous computational and analytical properties of the Stiefel manifold. Nonetheless, the interplay of nonsmoothness alongside orthogonality constraints introduces substantial challenges to current Riemannian methods, including scalability, parallelizability, complicated subproblems, and cumulative numerical errors that threaten feasibility. In this paper, we take a retraction-free primal-dual approach and propose a linearized smoothing augmented Lagrangian method specifically designed for nonsmooth and nonconvex optimization with orthogonality constraints. Our proposed method is single-loop and free of subproblem solving. We establish its iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for finding $ε$-KKT points, matching the best-known results in the Riemannian optimization literature. Additionally, by invoking the standard Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we demonstrate asymptotic sequential convergence of the proposed algorithm. Numerical experiments on both smooth and nonsmooth orthogonal constrained problems demonstrate the superior computational efficiency and scalability of the proposed method compared with state-of-the-art algorithms.

</details>


### [147] [On the optimal relaxation parameter of graph-based splitting methods for subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04206)
*Francisco J. Aragón-Artacho,César López-Pastor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于图的分裂算法在线性子空间交集问题中的行为，证明了当图与子图重合时，最优松弛参数恰好为1，将Douglas-Rachford算法的已知结果推广到更广泛的算法类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于图的分裂算法在线性子空间交集问题中的行为特性，特别是确定最优松弛参数，将Douglas-Rachford算法的已知结果扩展到更广泛的算法类别。

Method: 使用基于图的分裂算法框架，该框架包含Douglas-Rachford等经典方法。算法由连通图和子图定义。分析依赖于等平均线性算子的性质，这些算子定义为等距映射和恒等映射的平均，并具有特定的范数对称性。

Result: 主要结果表明，当图与子图重合时，最优松弛参数恰好为1。这扩展了Douglas-Rachford算法的已知结果，为更广泛的基于图的分裂算法类提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 论文成功将Douglas-Rachford算法的最优松弛参数结果推广到更广泛的基于图的分裂算法类，为这类算法在线性子空间交集问题中的应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the behavior of the family of graph-based splitting algorithms specialized to the problem of finding a point in the intersection of linear subspaces. The algorithms in this family, which encompasses several classical methods such as the Douglas-Rachford algorithm, are defined by a connected graph and a subgraph. Our main result establishes that when the graph and subgraph coincide, the optimal relaxation parameter is exactly $1$, thereby extending known results for the Douglas-Rachford algorithm to a much broader class of methods. Our analysis hinges on some properties of iso-averaged linear operators, which are defined as the average of an isometry and the identity, and are characterized by a specific symmetry of the norm of their relaxation.

</details>


### [148] [Smooth and Exact Parameterization of Continuous-time Signal Temporal Logic Specifications for Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04245)
*Samet Uzun,Behçet Açıkmeşe*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种连续时间信号时序逻辑（CT-STL）的平滑参数化方法，用于非凸轨迹优化，该方法在底层数值积分方案的精度范围内是完备的，能够保证连续时间约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹优化方法通常在离散采样节点上强制执行CT-STL规范，这会导致"always"谓词在连续时间上无法保证满足（如避障约束），而"eventually"谓词则因节点限制产生保守性。此外，标准定量语义存在局部性和梯度掩蔽问题，不利于基于梯度的求解器。

Method: 提出密集时间评估方法：1) 对"always"谓词保证连续时间满足；2) 对"eventually"谓词允许在规定区间内任意时间满足，消除节点诱导的保守性。这两种构造作为处理更复杂CT-STL公式（包括until规范）的基础模块。该方法解决了标准定量语义的局部性和梯度掩蔽问题，为基于梯度的求解器提供更有利的优化景观。

Result: 虽然密集时间评估在离散化过程中增加了函数评估次数，但允许使用更粗的时间网格而不牺牲安全性或逻辑保真度，从而降低了非凸规划的维度（通常是轨迹生成成本的主要因素）。在受复杂连续时间until规范约束的敏捷四旋翼飞行问题上展示了数值有效性和语义精确性。

Conclusion: 该方法为连续时间STL规范提供了在数值积分精度范围内完备的参数化，解决了现有离散时间方法的局限性，通过密集时间评估实现了连续时间约束满足，同时改善了优化问题的可解性，在保持安全性和逻辑保真度的前提下降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: This paper presents a smooth parameterization of continuous-time Signal Temporal Logic (CT-STL) specifications for nonconvex trajectory optimization that is sound and complete up to the accuracy of the underlying numerical integration scheme. CT-STL provides a natural framework for encoding rich temporal and logical task requirements, but existing trajectory-optimization formulations typically enforce such specifications only at discrete sampling nodes. In contrast, the proposed method evaluates specifications in dense time, thereby guaranteeing continuous-time satisfaction of always predicates, which is critical for path constraints such as obstacle avoidance, while eliminating the node-induced conservatism of eventually predicates by allowing satisfaction at any time within the prescribed interval. These two dense-time constructions also serve as the main building blocks for handling more general CT-STL formulas, including complex until specifications. Furthermore, the proposed parameterization resolves the locality and gradient-masking issues inherent in standard quantitative semantics, yielding a more favorable landscape for gradient-based solvers. Although dense-time evaluation introduces additional function evaluations during discretization, it also permits substantially coarser temporal grids without sacrificing safety or logical fidelity. This, in turn, reduces the dimension of the resulting nonconvex program, which is often the dominant factor in trajectory-generation cost. The numerical effectiveness and semantic exactness of the proposed framework are demonstrated on an agile quadrotor flight problem subject to a complex continuous-time until specification. The implementation is available at https:// github.com/UW-ACL/TrajOpt_CT-STL

</details>


### [149] [On the Regularity of Generalized Conjugate Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04301)
*Konstantinos Oikonomidis,Emanuel Laude,Panagiotis Patrinos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了由一般耦合函数诱导的广义共轭函数及其相关广义邻近映射的正则性性质，建立了确保局部单值性、连续性、Lipschitz连续性和可微性的可验证条件，并推导了导数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义共轭函数和广义邻近映射的正则性性质，为优化理论和变分分析提供理论基础，特别是在非光滑优化和广义方程求解中具有重要意义。

Method: 基于广义方程的非光滑隐函数定理，利用图式局部化和二阶变分工具，建立广义邻近映射的正则性条件，并将这些性质传递到广义共轭函数。

Result: 获得了确保广义邻近映射局部单值性、连续性、Lipschitz连续性和可微性的可验证条件，推导了广义共轭函数的显式导数公式，并建立了广义共轭函数严格二次可微的条件。

Conclusion: 论文为广义共轭函数和广义邻近映射的正则性分析提供了系统框架，所建立的工具和结果在非光滑优化和变分分析中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We investigate regularity properties of generalized conjugate functions induced by a general coupling function and the associated generalized proximal mapping. Our main results provide verifiable conditions ensuring local single-valuedness, continuity, Lipschitz continuity, and differentiability of the generalized proximal mapping, and transfer these properties to generalized conjugates providing explicit derivative formulas. These results are based on a nonsmooth implicit function theorem for generalized equations, relying on graphical localizations and second-order variational tools. Beyond first-order regularity, we also derive conditions under which generalized conjugates are strictly twice differentiable.

</details>


### [150] [Analytic Non-Gaussian Confidence Boundary Method for Chance-Constrained Trajectory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04304)
*Ethan Burnett,Spencer Boone*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种非高斯置信边界参数化技术，用于处理非线性系统中偏离高斯假设的随机控制问题，通过高阶统计矩扰动协方差预测边界，在航天器机动问题中优于线性协方差方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机会约束控制算法通常假设车辆状态不确定性服从高斯统计，但高度非线性系统会破坏高斯性，挑战标准方法。本文针对显著偏离高斯假设的情况开发非高斯置信边界参数化技术。

Method: 将真实置信边界视为协方差预测边界的扰动，从计算的高阶统计矩推导扰动边界几何。针对"香蕉形分布"（如轨道力学问题），使用偏度和峰度张量简单参数化置信边界，应用于二体动力学中的脉冲随机航天器机动目标问题。

Result: 算法实现优于标准线性协方差方法，在计算满足非高斯分布特定概率边界的控制参数方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 提出的非高斯置信边界参数化技术有效处理了非线性系统中偏离高斯假设的随机控制问题，为轨道力学等领域的非高斯不确定性管理提供了实用方法。

Abstract: Standard chance constrained control algorithms typically rely on the assumption that uncertainties in vehicle states obey Gaussian statistics. Highly nonlinear systems tend to disrupt Gaussianity, challenging standard chance-constrained control methods. This paper develops a non-Gaussian confidence boundary parameterization technique for such cases where the problem departs appreciably from the Gaussian assumption. The approach is to consider the true confidence boundary as a perturbation of the one predicted from covariance, deriving perturbed boundary geometry from computed higher-order statistical moments. Applying this technique to so-called "banana-shaped distributions" (found e.g. in orbital mechanics problems) enables a simple parameterization of the confidence boundary using the skew and kurtosis tensors. The method is then applied to an impulsive stochastic spacecraft maneuver targeting problem in two-body dynamics. An algorithmic implementation outperforms a standard linear covariance-based approach in computing control parameters satisfying certain probabilistic bounds on the non-Gaussian distribution.

</details>


### [151] [Partial health status observability and time horizon uncertainty in mean-field game epidemiological models$^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04305)
*Carlos Doebeli,Alexander Vladimirsky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于平均场博弈的流行病学模型，处理免疫随时间衰减或瞬时消失两种情况，通过求解前向-后向MFG系统来优化个体接触率决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流行病模型未考虑个体基于自身免疫状态和流行病学情况的理性决策，需要开发能够处理免疫衰减（可观测）和免疫瞬时消失（不可观测）两种情况的建模框架

Method: 建立平均场博弈流行病模型，包含免疫结构种群的对流-反应方程和相应值函数的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，通过求解近似ODE系统的两点边值问题来高效计算

Result: 开发了高效求解前向-后向MFG系统的方法，能够处理两种免疫变化模式，并可扩展到处理规划期初的不确定性

Conclusion: 平均场博弈为流行病学建模提供了新框架，能够捕捉个体理性决策与群体动态的相互作用，所提数值方法有效解决了相关计算挑战

Abstract: We introduce Mean-Field Game (MFG) epidemiological models, in which immunity either wanes with time in a fully observable way or disappears instantaneously with no direct observation (making a previously recovered individual fully susceptible again without realizing it). Both interpretations create computational challenges for rational noninfected individuals deciding on their contact rates based on their personal current immunity state and the changing epidemiological situation. Both require solving a forward-backward MFG system that includes PDEs (an advection-reaction equation for the immunity-structured population and a Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for the corresponding value function). We show how this can be done efficiently by solving a two-point boundary value problem for a system of approximating ODEs. We also show how the same approach can be extended to handle an initial uncertainty in the planning horizon.

</details>


### [152] [MPC and System Identification with Differentiable Physics: Fluid System and Particle Beam Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04327)
*Alan Williams,Alp Sunol*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可微分物理模拟器的同时控制和参数估计框架，通过自动微分获取梯度，在线优化控制输入和物理参数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统模型预测控制依赖显式代数模型的局限性，利用可微分模拟器处理复杂物理系统，实现控制与参数估计的联合优化

Method: 采用滚动时域控制框架，通过可微分物理模拟器获取梯度，使用自动微分进行基于仿真的优化，在线更新控制输入和物理参数

Result: 在流体流动问题和粒子加速器两个挑战性应用中展示了框架的有效性，实现了同时控制和参数估计

Conclusion: 提出的可微分模拟器方法为复杂物理系统的控制提供了新途径，能够处理传统MPC难以建模的系统，实现控制与参数估计的联合实时优化

Abstract: We consider the problem of simultaneous control and parameter estimation when the model is available only as a differentiable physics simulator. We propose a receding-horizon control framework in which a model predictive control (MPC) objective is optimized using gradients obtained by differentiating through the simulator, while physical parameters are updated online using measurement data. Unlike classical MPC, which relies on explicit algebraic models, our approach treats the dynamics as a computational object and performs simulation-based optimization using automatic differentiation. A shared differentiable model enables joint, real-time optimization of control inputs and physical parameters. We present two preliminary examples to demonstrate the proposed framework on two challenging applications: a fluid flow problem and a particle accelerator.

</details>


### [153] [Polynomial iteration complexity of a path-following smoothing Newton method for symmetric cone programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04376)
*Yu-Hong Dai,Ruoyu Diao,Xin-Wei Liu,Rui-Jin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路径跟随平滑牛顿法(PFSNM)，解决了对称锥规划中平滑牛顿方法多项式迭代复杂度的长期开放问题，通过引入自协调凸凹的简化平滑障碍增广拉格朗日函数，实现了与内点法相同的O(√ν ln(1/ε))迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称锥规划中平滑牛顿方法能否达到多项式迭代复杂度是一个长期未解决的开放问题。主要困难在于缺乏类似内点法中自协调凸框架的理论基础。本文旨在正面回答这个问题，为平滑牛顿方法建立多项式迭代复杂度的理论保证。

Method: 引入简化平滑障碍增广拉格朗日函数，证明其具有自协调凸凹性质，将经典自协调理论扩展到凸设置之外。基于此等价性，提出路径跟随平滑牛顿法，利用简化SBAL函数诱导的中心路径和邻域估计牛顿减量，进行路径跟随分析。

Result: 证明了PFSNM方法达到O(√ν ln(1/ε))的迭代复杂度，与内点法的最佳短步长界限相匹配。在标准基准测试上的数值结果表明，PFSNM与多个知名内点求解器具有竞争力，为多项式迭代复杂度提供了计算支持。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了对称锥规划中平滑牛顿方法多项式迭代复杂度的开放问题，通过引入自协调凸凹的简化SBAL函数和相应的路径跟随方法，建立了与内点法相当的理论保证和计算性能。

Abstract: Whether polynomial iteration complexity can be established for smoothing Newton methods (SNMs) in symmetric cone programming (SCP) remains a long-standing open problem. A key difficulty lies in the lack of an analogue of the self-concordant convex framework in interior-point methods (IPMs). In this paper, we answer this question affirmatively. We introduce a reduced smoothing barrier augmented Lagrangian (SBAL) function and prove that it is self-concordant convex-concave, which extends the classical self-concordant theory beyond the convex setting. Furthermore, we show that the parameterized smooth equations associated with SNMs are equivalent to the first-order optimality conditions of a minimax problem whose objective is the reduced SBAL function. Motivated by this equivalence, we propose a path-following smoothing Newton method (PFSNM). The reduced SBAL function induces a central path and an associated neighborhood, which provide estimates of the Newton decrement needed for the path-following analysis. As a result, the method is proven to achieve an iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}( \sqrtν \ln(1/\varepsilon) )$, matching the best-known short-step bound for IPMs. Numerical results on standard benchmarks show that PFSNM is competitive with several well-known interior-point solvers, providing computational support for the polynomial iteration complexity.

</details>


### [154] [An Alternating Primal Heuristic for Nonconvex MIQCQP with Dynamic Convexification and Parallel Local Branching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04417)
*Yongzheng Dai,Chen Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于非凸混合整数二次约束二次规划的新型原始启发式算法，基于动态调整的凸近似和可行性泵框架，结合并行局部分支，在QPLIB实例上取得显著效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸混合整数二次约束二次规划在实际应用中广泛存在但求解困难，现有方法难以高效找到可行解或改进已知解，需要开发更有效的原始启发式算法

Method: 基于动态调整凸近似的可行性泵式交替启发式算法，根据问题结构调整近似，并结合并行局部分支来进一步精化检测到的解

Result: 在QPLIB实例上的计算实验验证了方法有效性：解决了3个先前未解决的实例，在15个实例上5分钟内改进了已知最优解

Conclusion: 该方法为非凸MIQCQP提供了一种有效的原始启发式算法，在计算竞赛中表现优异，能够快速找到高质量可行解

Abstract: We develop a novel primal heuristic for nonconvex Mixed-Integer Quadratically Constrained Quadratic Programs (MIQCQPs). The method is built around a convex approximation that is dynamically adjusted within a feasibility-pump-style alternating heuristic. Approximations are adjusted based on the structure of the MIQCQP instance. Additionally, parallelized local branching is incorporated to further refine detected solutions. This paper builds upon the second-place finalist submission in the 2025 Land-Doig MIP Computational Competition. Our results are validated with computational experiments on instances from QPLIB, finding feasible solutions for three previously unsolved cases and improving the best-known solutions for fifteen instances within five minutes of runtime.

</details>


### [155] [Viscosity Solutions of Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman Equations for Control Systems Driven by Teugels Martingales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04478)
*Yongpeng Lin,Qingxin Meng,Maoning Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有随机系数的离散时间两人非零和线性二次随机博弈，通过凸变分分析建立了开环纳什均衡存在性的充要条件，并发展了全耦合的前向-后向随机差分方程和非对称随机Riccati方程系统来显式刻画均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有随机系数的离散时间两人非零和线性二次随机博弈问题，解决在权重矩阵不定时经典一阶条件不再充分的问题，建立随机环境下开环纳什均衡的完整理论框架。

Method: 采用凸变分分析方法，引入全局非负性条件恢复充分性；发展全耦合前向-后向随机差分方程(FBSΔEs)和非对称随机Riccati方程系统；提供保证相关算子可逆性的充分条件（Riccati矩阵算子半正定和结构非退化性）。

Result: 建立了开环纳什均衡存在性的充要条件；实现了随机Hamilton系统的解耦；获得了开环纳什均衡策略的闭环反馈表示的良好适定性；处理了完全随机系数导致的完全非线性高阶后向随机差分方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有随机系数的离散时间两人非零和线性二次随机博弈提供了完整的理论框架，解决了权重矩阵不定时的充分性问题，发展了随机Riccati方程方法，显著区别于确定性设定中的代数Riccati方程。

Abstract: This paper studies discrete-time two-person nonzero-sum linear quadratic stochastic games with random coefficients. Using convex variational analysis, we derive necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of open-loop Nash equilibria. When weighting matrices are indefinite, the classical first-order conditions are no longer sufficient for optimality; we introduce a global nonnegativity condition to restore sufficiency, which becomes a cornerstone of the subsequent analysis. To characterize the equilibria explicitly, we develop fully coupled forward-backward stochastic difference equations and a system of non-symmetric stochastic Riccati equations (FBS$Δ$Es) with constraints. that decouple the stochastic Hamiltonian system. A key technical contribution is the provision of sufficient conditions -- positive semidefiniteness of the Riccati matrices operators and structural non-degeneracy -- that guarantee the invertibility of a related operator, ensuring the well-posedness of the closed-loop feedback representation of the open-loop Nash equilibrium strategies. A distinctive feature of this work is the presence of fully random coefficients, which leads to fully nonlinear higher-order backward stochastic difference equations in the Riccati framework, in contrast to the algebraic Riccati equations in the deterministic setting.

</details>


### [156] [A Near-Optimal Total Complexity for the Inexact Accelerated Proximal Gradient Method via Quadratic Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04551)
*Hongda Li,Xianfu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有锥多面体结构的复合优化问题，证明了双循环结构中的不精确加速近端梯度法(IAPG)能达到O(ln(1/ε)/√ε)的总复杂度，改进了现有最佳复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复合优化问题min_x{f(x)+ω(Ax)}的高效求解方法，其中f是L-Lipschitz光滑函数，ω是锥多面体函数。现有不精确加速近端梯度法(IAPG)的复杂度有待改进，特别是当ω具有特殊结构时。

Method: 采用双循环结构的不精确加速近端梯度法(IAPG)。外层循环执行加速近端梯度法，内层循环求解不精确近端子问题。关键理论工具是证明不精确近端子问题对偶的二次增长条件，该条件源于ω的锥多面体结构，确保了内层近端梯度循环的线性收敛性。

Result: 当ω是锥多面体函数时，IAPG在双循环结构下达到总复杂度O(ln(1/ε)/√ε)，以对ω的凸共轭近端算子和f的梯度调用次数衡量，以获得ε最优函数值。这改进了已知的IAPG最佳复杂度。数值实验在鲁棒TV-ℓ2信号恢复问题上验证了快速收敛性。

Conclusion: 对于具有锥多面体结构的复合优化问题，双循环IAPG方法能够实现改进的复杂度界限。关键理论贡献是建立了不精确近端子问题对偶的二次增长条件，该条件源于ω的锥多面体性质，确保了内层循环的线性收敛，从而降低了总复杂度。

Abstract: We consider the optimization problem $\min_{x\in \mathbb R^n}{F(x):=f(x)+ω(Ax)}$, where $f$ is an $L$-Lipschitz smooth function, and $ω$ is a proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex function.
