We prove in this paper that when $ω$ is a conic polyhedral function, the inexact accelerated proximal gradient method (IAPG), employed in a double-loop structure, achieves a total complexity of $\mathcal O(\ln(1/\varepsilon)/\sqrt{\varepsilon})$ measured by the total number of calls to the proximal operator of the convex conjugate $ω^\star$ and the gradient of $f$ to achieve $\varepsilon$-optimality in function value. To the best of our knowledge, this improves upon the best-known complexity for IAPG. The key theoretical ingredient is a quadratic growth condition on the dual of the inexact proximal problem, which arises from the conic polyhedral structure of $ω$ and implies linear convergence of the inner proximal gradient loop. To validate these findings, we conduct numerical experiments on a robust TV-$\ell_2$ signal recovery problem, demonstrating fast convergence.

</details>


### [157] [Dividend ratcheting and capital injection under the Cramér-Lundberg model: Strong solution and optimal strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04641)
*Chonghu Guan,Zuo Quan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了Cramér-Lundberg模型下具有分红率棘轮约束和资本注入的保险公司最优分红问题，通过概率和PDE方法解决了相关的HJB方程，首次给出了该问题的完整解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究保险公司在Cramér-Lundberg模型下的最优分红问题，其中分红率受到棘轮约束（随时间非递减），并且公司可以以比例成本注入资本以避免破产。该问题涉及自路径依赖控制约束、成本资本注入和跳扩散动态，在数学上具有挑战性。

Method: 采用系统的概率和PDE方法：通过离散化可接受分红率空间，构造一系列逼近的体制切换常积分-微分方程组；通过先验估计和极限论证，证明在合适空间中强解的存在唯一性；利用正则性结果刻画最优分红策略。

Result: 证明了HJB方程强解的存在唯一性，能够通过切换自由边界刻画最优分红策略，并构造了显式的最优反馈控制策略。这是首次为Cramér-Lundberg模型下具有资本注入的分红棘轮问题提供完整解（包括价值函数和可实施的最优策略）。

Conclusion: 该工作超越了标准粘性解框架，为最优随机控制理论提供了严格的数学基础，为经济学中的分红政策设计提供了理论基础。方法具有系统性，正则性结果对策略表征至关重要。

Abstract: We consider an optimal dividend payout problem for an insurance company whose surplus follows the classical Cramér-Lundberg model. The dividend rate is subject to a ratcheting constraint (i.e., it must be nondecreasing over time), and the company may inject capital at a proportional cost to avoid ruin. This problem gives rise to a stochastic control problem with a self-path-dependent control constraint, costly capital injections, and jump-diffusion dynamics. The associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation is a partial integro-differential variational inequality featuring both a nonlocal integral term and a gradient constraint.
  We develop a systematic probabilistic and PDE-based approach to solve this HJB equation. By discretizing the space of admissible dividend rates, we construct a sequence of approximating regime-switching systems of ordinary integro-differential equations. Through careful a priori estimates and a limiting argument, we prove the existence and uniqueness of a \emph{strong solution} in a suitable space. This regularity result is fundamental: it allows us to characterize the optimal dividend policy via a switching free boundary and to construct an explicit optimal feedback control strategy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first complete solution -- comprising both the value function and an implementable optimal strategy -- for a dividend ratcheting problem with capital injection under the Cramér-Lundberg model. Our work advances the mathematical theory of optimal stochastic control beyond the standard viscosity solution framework, providing a rigorous foundation for dividend policy design in economics.

</details>


### [158] [Determinant Dynamics under Low-Rank Perturbations: A Unified Framework for Singular Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04650)
*Robert Vrabel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文建立了一个统一的解析框架，用于处理方阵在有限秩扰动下的行列式恒等式，无需可逆性假设。基于伴随矩阵的加法表示，推导了递归和乘法表达式，揭示了行列式量随秩一更新的演化结构，并扩展到奇异矩阵情况，建立了与系统理论概念的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的行列式恒等式通常依赖于矩阵可逆性假设，这限制了在奇异矩阵情况下的应用。需要建立一个统一的解析框架，能够处理有限秩扰动下的行列式演化，特别是在奇异矩阵情况下，以连接矩阵扰动理论和系统理论概念。

Method: 采用基于伴随矩阵的加法表示方法，而非传统的逆矩阵表示。该方法自然扩展到奇异矩阵，并推导出行列式和log-行列式在连续秩一更新下的递归和乘法表达式。针对奇异情况，系统性地扩展了Drazin逆和伪行列式，得到封闭形式的恒等式，分离了非零谱的贡献。

Result: 获得了广义行列式公式，可视为矩阵行列式引理的奇异对应物。分析了低秩扰动的谱影响，得到了控制特征值移动和稳定性保持的显式条件。建立了矩阵扰动理论与系统理论概念的直接联系，特别是证明了可控性格拉姆矩阵的伪行列式允许乘法分解，显式量化了连续输入下可达子空间的增量扩展。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限秩扰动下的行列式恒等式提供了统一的解析方法，无需可逆性假设。揭示了行列式量作为独立方向累积度量的结构解释，并建立了与系统理论中信息积累、不确定性减少和可达性概念的统一联系，适用于满秩和秩亏线性系统。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified analytical framework for determinant identities under finite-rank perturbations of square matrices that remains valid without invertibility assumptions. In contrast to classical inverse-based formulations, the approach is based on an adjugate-driven additive representation, which extends naturally to singular matrices and yields explicit, non-asymptotic formulas. Building on this representation, we derive recursive and multiplicative expressions describing the evolution of determinant and log-determinant quantities under successive rank-one updates. These results reveal a structural interpretation in which determinant-based quantities evolve as cumulative measures of independent directions, providing a precise decomposition of incremental contributions. To address the singular case, we develop a systematic extension based on the Drazin inverse and the pseudodeterminant, leading to closed-form identities that isolate the contribution of the nonzero spectrum. In particular, we obtain a generalized determinant formula that can be viewed as a singular counterpart of the matrix determinant lemma. The spectral impact of low-rank perturbations is analyzed, yielding explicit conditions governing eigenvalue shifts and stability preservation. The proposed framework establishes a direct analytical link between matrix perturbation theory and system-theoretic concepts. In particular, we show that the pseudodeterminant of controllability Gramians admits a multiplicative decomposition that explicitly quantifies the incremental expansion of the reachable subspace under successive inputs. This leads to a unified interpretation of information accumulation, uncertainty reduction, and reachability in both full-rank and rank-deficient linear systems.

</details>


### [159] [Exact Methods for the Generalized Multiple Strip Packing Problem with Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04740)
*Hyunwoo Lee,Taesu Cheong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文首次研究具有异质单位面积成本的广义多条带装箱问题，提出两种精确整数规划公式和一个有效的Benders分解算法BendM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中多个目标（如总面积、相同条带的总高度、完工时间）被分开研究，缺乏统一框架。本文首次引入成本加权面积目标，将这些问题统一起来，并扩展到具有不同宽度和异质单位面积成本的多条带场景。

Method: 提出两种精确整数规划公式：1）基于近期工作的大M公式；2）将早期单条带方法扩展到多条带异质场景的正常位置公式。针对正常位置公式，开发了精确的Benders分解算法BendM（多条带的Benders方法）。

Result: 在从标准条带装箱基准生成的180个实例上进行全面计算实验，比较两种公式，并证明BendM在三种成本结构下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次提出具有异质单位面积成本的广义多条带装箱问题，建立了统一框架，开发了有效的精确算法BendM，为解决这类复杂装箱问题提供了新方法。

Abstract: We study the Generalized Multiple Strip Packing Problem (GMSPP) with heterogeneous per-unit-area costs, in which rectangular items of fixed dimensions must be packed without overlap into multiple open-ended strips of different widths, each incurring a cost proportional to the area used. This cost-weighted area objective is introduced here for the first time and unifies several objectives studied separately in the literature, including total area, total height for identical strips, and makespan. We propose two exact integer programming formulations for this problem: a big-M formulation adapted from recent work, and a normal-position formulation extending an earlier single-strip approach to multiple heterogeneous strips. For the normal-position formulation, we develop an exact Benders decomposition algorithm, called BendM (Benders' Method for Multiple strips). Comprehensive computational experiments on 180 instances derived from standard strip-packing benchmarks compare both formulations and demonstrate the effectiveness of BendM across three cost structures.

</details>


### [160] [Extremum Seeking of Static Maps in the Presence of Unknown Large Time-Varying Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04754)
*Adam Jbara,Emilia Fridman,Xuefei Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对具有未知时变大测量延迟的n维静态二次映射的离散时间无偏极值搜索算法，首次实现了对未知大时变延迟的鲁棒性，并达到无偏指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极值搜索算法在处理大延迟时通常局限于已知常数或缓慢变化延迟，这种限制在实际应用中不够灵活。需要开发能够处理未知大时变延迟的鲁棒算法。

Method: 采用频率为√ε量级的抖动信号，其中ε是实时估计器动态中的小参数。通过基于无延迟变换的平均分析提供定性和定量结果，并假设Hessian矩阵不确定但处于已知范围内。

Result: 实现了对任意大未知时变有界延迟的鲁棒性，获得无偏指数收敛。较大延迟导致较慢收敛，但总能找到合适的ES参数。数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 首次提出了能够处理未知大时变延迟的离散时间无偏极值搜索算法，通过适当的参数选择，可以在任何大未知时变有界延迟下实现指数收敛，扩展了ES算法的应用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the discrete-time unbiased extremum seeking (ES) algorithm for n-dimensional (nD) static quadratic maps in the presence of unknown time-varying measurement delays bounded by known constants which can be large. The existing ES results in the presence of large delays are usually confined to known constant or slowly-varying delays, which is restrictive. We provide the first ES algorithm, which is robust with respect to unknown large time-varying delays. Moreover, we achieve the unbiased exponential convergence. We manage with such delays by choosing dithers with frequencies of the order of \sqrtε, where the small parameter ε > 0 appears in the dynamics of the real-time estimator. As expected, larger delays lead to a slower convergence. We provide qualitative and quantitative results based on the averaging analysis via delay-free transformation. For the quantitative bounds on the controller parameters that ensure the exponential unbiased convergence of the ES system, we assume that the Hessian of the map is uncertain and lies within a known range. Differently from its continuous-time counterpart, the small parameter in the discrete-time case defines the decay rate of the estimation error system, making a quantitative bound on this parameter particularly important. We present also constructive conditions for the practical stability of the classical ES system. Our results are semi-global for globally quadratic maps, while for locally quadratic static maps, we provide a bound on the region of convergence. Our analysis shows that appropriate ES parameters can be found for any large unknown time-varying bounded delay. A numerical example highlights the efficiency of the method.

</details>


### [161] [Collaborative Altruistic Safety in Coupled Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04772)
*Brooks A. Butler,Xiao Tan,Aaron D. Ames,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于生态利他主义模型的协作控制屏障函数框架，通过Hamilton规则实现多智能体系统在动态耦合下的安全约束协同执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决动态耦合多智能体系统中个体安全约束难以协同执行的问题，传统方法在强耦合下可能失效，需要新的协作安全框架

Method: 基于生态利他主义模型开发协作控制屏障函数，引入基于Hamilton规则的利他安全条件，构建分布式优化框架实现安全约束协同执行

Result: 在简化编队控制场景中验证了方法的有效性，提高了系统安全约束的可行性和鲁棒性

Conclusion: 提出的协作控制屏障函数框架能够有效处理动态耦合多智能体系统的安全约束问题，通过利他主义机制增强了系统整体安全性

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework for ensuring safety in dynamically coupled multi-agent systems through collaborative control. Drawing inspiration from ecological models of altruism, we develop collaborative control barrier functions that allow agents to cooperatively enforce individual safety constraints under coupling dynamics. We introduce an altruistic safety condition based on the so-called Hamilton's rule, enabling agents to trade off their own safety to support higher-priority neighbors. By incorporating these conditions into a distributed optimization framework, we demonstrate increased feasibility and robustness in maintaining system-wide safety. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is illustrated through simulation in a simplified formation control scenario.

</details>


### [162] [Sample Complexity for Markov Decision Processes and Stochastic Optimal Control with Static Risk Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04795)
*Cristian Chávez,Yan Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种针对静态风险度量的状态增强方法，使得在增强空间中能够推导动态规划方程，并分析马尔可夫决策过程和随机最优控制问题的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对静态风险度量应用于总成本时，传统动态规划方法难以直接应用的问题，需要一种方法能够将静态风险度量转化为适合动态规划的形式，从而分析马尔可夫决策过程和随机最优控制问题的样本复杂度。

Method: 提出一种基本的状态增强方法，将静态风险度量应用于总成本的问题转化为增强空间中的动态规划问题。该方法适用于由φ-散度生成的分布鲁棒泛函，包括条件风险价值（CVaR）。

Result: 在增强空间中推导出动态规划方程，使得能够分析有限时域和无限时域设置下马尔可夫决策过程和随机最优控制问题的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提出的状态增强方法为静态风险度量在动态决策问题中的应用提供了理论框架，使得能够通过动态规划分析样本复杂度，特别适用于分布鲁棒优化问题。

Abstract: We present an elementary state augmentation method for a class of static risk measure applied to the total cost for both Markov decision processes and stochastic optimal control, such that dynamic programming equations can be derived on the augmented space. Through this we discuss the sample complexities of these two problems for both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon settings. We demonstrate the application of the proposed approach through studying distributionally robust functional generated by $φ$-divergences including conditional value-at-risk.

</details>


### [163] [Curvature batching gives single-exponential integer quadratic programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04851)
*Cinar Ari,Robert Hildebrand*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出首个整数二次规划的单指数时间算法，通过曲率批处理技术将运行时间改进为(nL)^{O(n²)}·poly(φ)，并给出凹整数最小化的显式参数复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数二次规划是组合优化的基本问题，但一般不定情况的计算复杂度较高。现有FPT算法参数化为n和最大系数L，但未给出显式运行时间。需要开发更高效的算法，特别是单指数时间算法。

Method: 采用曲率批处理技术：根据二次曲率的符号对核方向进行分类，当不存在负曲率方向时，所有梯度约束可以同时在一个批次中施加。这用单次多项式膨胀替代了顺序分支中的行列式平方链，剩余问题转化为整数线性规划。

Result: 得到首个整数二次规划的单指数时间算法，运行时间为(nL_A^n·Δ(A)·L_Q)^{O(n)}·poly(φ)，一般情况为(nL)^{O(n²)}·poly(φ)。对全幺模等结构化情况有改进，并给出了凹整数最小化的显式参数复杂度界。

Conclusion: 曲率批处理技术成功实现了整数二次规划的单指数时间算法，显著改进了现有结果。该方法为参数化算法设计提供了新思路，对结构化矩阵情况有进一步优化潜力。

Abstract: Integer Quadratic Programming (IQP), $\min\{x^T Q x + c^T x : Ax \le b,\, x\in\Z^n\}$, is a fundamental problem in combinatorial optimization. While the convex and concave special cases admit polynomial-time algorithms for fixed~$n$, the general indefinite case is considerably harder: it was only recently shown to lie in NP, and the FPT algorithm, due to Lokshtanov, establishes fixed-parameter tractability parameterized by $n$ and the largest coefficient~$L$ without giving an explicit running time.
  We give the first single-exponential algorithm for IQP, solving it in time $
  \bigl(n\,L^n_A\,Δ(A)\,L_Q\bigr)^{O(n)}\cdot\mathrm{poly}(\varphi), $ which is $(nL)^{O(n^2)}\cdot\mathrm{poly}(\varphi)$ in general using the same parameterization. We achieve improvements for structured cases like total unimodularity and further state explicit complexity results for a number of FPT algorithms and optimization problems.
  The single-exponential bound is achieved via curvature batching: we classify kernel directions by the sign of their quadratic curvature and observe that when no negative-curvature direction exists, all gradient constraints can be imposed simultaneously in a single batch. This replaces the chain of determinant squarings inherent in sequential branching with a single polynomial inflation, after which the remaining problem is an ILP.
  As a secondary contribution, we give an explicit bound for concave integer minimization over a polytope $\{Ax \le b\} \cap \Z^n$ whose parametric complexity depends only on the constraint matrix~$A$ and is independent of the right-hand side~$b$.

</details>


### [164] [Muon Dynamics as a Spectral Wasserstein Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04891)
*Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了深度学习中梯度归一化的谱几何方法，引入了谱Wasserstein距离族，将梯度下降、Muon和Schatten型归一化方案统一在一个概率测度框架中，并建立了相应的最优传输理论和梯度流理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度架构中参数自然分组为矩阵或块，谱归一化比坐标欧几里得归一化更忠实。Muon是主要动机示例，但需要更广泛的谱归一化规则理论框架，在参数建模为概率测度的平均场机制下进行研究。

Method: 引入由正半定矩阵范数γ索引的谱Wasserstein距离族：迹范数恢复经典二次Wasserstein距离，算子范数恢复Muon几何，中间Schatten范数在两者之间插值。发展了静态Kantorovich公式，证明了与W2的比较界，推导了最大-最小表示，获得了条件Brenier定理。对于高斯边缘分布，问题简化为协方差矩阵上的约束优化，扩展了Bures公式，并为Schatten族中的交换协方差提供了闭式解。

Result: 对于单调范数（包括所有Schatten情况），证明了静态和动态Benamou-Brenier公式的等价性，推导出所得传输成本是固定维度下与W2等价的真正度量，并证明诱导的高斯协方差成本也是度量。将相关的归一化连续性方程解释为谱Wasserstein梯度流，确定了其精确有限粒子对应物为归一化矩阵流，获得了第一个测地凸性结果，并展示了正齐次平均场模型如何在球面上诱导谱不平衡传输。

Conclusion: 该研究为深度学习中梯度归一化的谱几何方法提供了统一的理论框架，将梯度下降、Muon和Schatten型方案统一在谱Wasserstein距离的概念下，建立了完整的最优传输理论、度量性质和梯度流解释，为深度学习的优化理论提供了新的数学基础。

Abstract: Gradient normalization is central in deep-learning optimization because it stabilizes training and reduces sensitivity to scale. For deep architectures, parameters are naturally grouped into matrices or blocks, so spectral normalizations are often more faithful than coordinatewise Euclidean ones; Muon is the main motivating example of this paper. More broadly, we study a family of spectral normalization rules, ranging from ordinary gradient descent to Muon and intermediate Schatten-type schemes, in a mean-field regime where parameters are modeled by probability measures. We introduce a family of Spectral Wasserstein distances indexed by a norm gamma on positive semidefinite matrices. The trace norm recovers the classical quadratic Wasserstein distance, the operator norm recovers the Muon geometry, and intermediate Schatten norms interpolate between them. We develop the static Kantorovich formulation, prove comparison bounds with W2, derive a max-min representation, and obtain a conditional Brenier theorem. For Gaussian marginals, the problem reduces to a constrained optimization on covariance matrices, extending the Bures formula and yielding a closed form for commuting covariances in the Schatten family. For monotone norms, including all Schatten cases, we prove the equivalence between the static and dynamic Benamou-Brenier formulations, deduce that the resulting transport cost is a genuine metric equivalent to W2 in fixed dimension, and show that the induced Gaussian covariance cost is also a metric. We then interpret the associated normalized continuity equation as a Spectral Wasserstein gradient flow, identify its exact finite-particle counterpart as a normalized matrix flow, obtain first geodesic-convexity results, and show how positively homogeneous mean-field models induce a spectral unbalanced transport on the sphere.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Scaling DPPs for RAG: Density Meets Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03240)
*Xun Sun,Baiheng Xie,Li Huang,Qiang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScalDPP：一种基于行列式点过程的多样性感知检索机制，通过轻量级P-Adapter和多样性边际损失优化RAG中的上下文密度与多样性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG管道通过相关性排序构建上下文，但忽略了检索候选之间的交互作用，导致冗余上下文稀释信息密度并无法呈现互补证据。需要同时优化密度和多样性，确保基础证据既信息密集又覆盖多样。

Method: 提出ScalDPP：1) 通过轻量级P-Adapter将行列式点过程(DPPs)集成到检索机制中，实现可扩展的块间依赖建模和互补上下文选择；2) 开发集合级目标函数Diverse Margin Loss(DML)，强制真实互补证据链在DPP几何下主导任何同等大小的冗余替代方案。

Result: 实验结果表明ScalDPP的优越性，在实践中证实了核心论点。

Conclusion: ScalDPP通过引入DPPs和DML损失，有效解决了RAG中上下文冗余问题，实现了密度和多样性的联合优化，提升了检索增强生成的质量。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances Large Language Models (LLMs) by grounding generation in external knowledge, yielding relevance responses that are aligned with factual evidence and evolving corpora. Standard RAG pipelines construct context through relevance ranking, performing point-wise scoring between the user query and each corpora chunk. This formulation, however, ignores interactions among retrieved candidates, leading to redundant contexts that dilute density and fail to surface complementary evidence. We argue that effective retrieval should optimize jointly for both density and diversity, ensuring the grounding evidence that is dense in information yet diverse in coverage. In this study, we propose ScalDPP, a diversity-aware retrieval mechanism for RAG that incorporates Determinantal Point Processes (DPPs) through a lightweight P-Adapter, enabling scalable modeling of inter-chunk dependencies and complementary context selection. In addition, we develop a novel set-level objective, Diverse Margin Loss (DML), that enforces ground-truth complementary evidence chains to dominate any equally sized redundant alternatives under DPP geometry. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of ScalDPP, substantiating our core statement in practice.

</details>


### [166] [DRAFT: Task Decoupled Latent Reasoning for Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03242)
*Lin Wang,Junfeng Fang,Dan Zhang,Fei Shen,Xiang Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRAFT提出了一种用于LLM智能体安全监控的潜在推理框架，通过解耦提取器和推理器的两阶段训练，在稀疏证据的长交互轨迹中实现更好的安全判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于输出的安全监控方法不适用于工具使用LLM智能体的长交互轨迹，因为风险关键证据稀疏，标准二元监督难以进行信用分配。

Method: DRAFT框架将安全判断解耦为两个可训练阶段：提取器将完整轨迹蒸馏为紧凑的连续潜在草稿，推理器联合关注草稿和原始轨迹来预测安全性，避免了显式的"总结-判断"流水线。

Result: 在ASSEBench和R-Judge等基准测试中，DRAFT持续优于强基线，准确率从63.27%（LoRA）提升到91.18%，学习到更具可分性的表示，消融实验显示提取器和推理器之间存在明确协同效应。

Conclusion: DRAFT表明，在读取前进行连续潜在推理是在稀疏证据的长上下文监督下实现鲁棒智能体安全的实用路径。

Abstract: The advent of tool-using LLM agents shifts safety monitoring from output moderation to auditing long, noisy interaction trajectories, where risk-critical evidence is sparse-making standard binary supervision poorly suited for credit assignment. To address this, we propose DRAFT (Task Decoupled Latent Reasoning for Agent Safety), a latent reasoning framework that decouples safety judgment into two trainable stages: an Extractor that distills the full trajectory into a compact continuous latent draft, and a Reasoner that jointly attends to the draft and the original trajectory to predict safety. DRAFT avoids lossy explicit summarize-then-judge pipelines by performing evidence aggregation in latent space, enabling end-to-end differentiable training.Across benchmarks including ASSEBench and R-Judge, DRAFT consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving accuracy from 63.27% (LoRA) to 91.18% averaged over benchmarks, and learns more separable representations. Ablations demonstrate a clear synergy between the Extractor and the Reasoner.Overall, DRAFT suggests that continuous latent reasoning prior to readout is a practical path to robust agent safety under long-context supervision with sparse evidence.

</details>


### [167] [Apparent Age Estimation: Challenges and Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03335)
*Justin Rainier Go,Lorenz Bernard Marqueses,Mikaella Kaye Martinez,John Kevin Patrick Sarmiento,Abien Fred Agarap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了表观年龄估计中的公平性问题，发现现有方法存在人口统计偏差，特别是对亚裔和非裔美国人群体的性能显著下降，需要结合本地化数据集和公平性验证协议来解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表观年龄估计在商业个性化中具有价值，但当前模型经常表现出人口统计偏差。研究旨在评估现有方法的准确性和公平性，揭示技术改进的局限性。

Method: 回顾了DEX方法的先前工作，应用了分布学习技术如均值-方差损失(MVL)和自适应均值-残差损失(AMRL)。使用IMDB-WIKI、APPA-REAL和FairFace数据集进行评估，通过UMAP嵌入和显著性图分析特征关注模式。

Result: AMRL实现了最先进的准确性，但精度和人口统计公平性之间存在权衡。UMAP嵌入显示清晰的年龄聚类，但显著性图表明不同人口统计群体间的特征关注不一致，导致亚裔和非裔美国人群体的性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 仅靠技术改进是不够的；准确且公平的表观年龄估计需要整合本地化和多样化的数据集，并严格遵守公平性验证协议。

Abstract: Apparent age estimation is a valuable tool for business personalization, yet current models frequently exhibit demographic biases. We review prior works on the DEX method by applying distribution learning techniques such as Mean-Variance Loss (MVL) and Adaptive Mean-Residue Loss (AMRL), and evaluate them in both accuracy and fairness. Using IMDB-WIKI, APPA-REAL, and FairFace, we demonstrate that while AMRL achieves state-of-the-art accuracy, trade-offs between precision and demographic equity persist. Despite clear age clustering in UMAP embeddings, our saliency maps indicate inconsistent feature focus across demographics, leading to significant performance degradation for Asian and African American populations. We argue that technical improvements alone are insufficient; accurate and fair apparent age estimation requires the integration of localized and diverse datasets, and strict adherence to fairness validation protocols.

</details>


### [168] [The limits of bio-molecular modeling with large language models : a cross-scale evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03361)
*Yaxin Xu,Yue Zhou,Tianyu Zhao,Fengwei An,Zhixiang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioMol-LLM-Bench：一个跨尺度生物分子基准测试框架，评估LLM在26个下游任务上的表现，发现思维链数据对生物任务帮助有限，混合Mamba-Attention架构更适合长序列，监督微调以泛化能力为代价提升专业化，LLM在分类任务表现良好但在回归任务较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在生物分子发现中的应用日益增多，但缺乏跨多尺度生物问题的系统性评估，以及对工具增强能力的严谨评估。需要揭示LLM性能与机理理解之间的系统性差距。

Method: 提出BioMol-LLM-Bench基准测试框架，包含26个下游任务，覆盖4个不同难度级别，集成计算工具进行更全面评估。在13个代表性模型上进行评估。

Result: 发现四个主要结论：1）思维链数据对生物任务帮助有限甚至可能降低性能；2）混合Mamba-Attention架构对长生物分子序列更有效；3）监督微调以泛化能力为代价提升专业化；4）当前LLM在分类任务表现良好但在挑战性回归任务上较弱。

Conclusion: 这些发现为未来基于LLM的分子系统建模提供了实用指导，强调了需要针对生物分子特性优化模型架构和训练策略。

Abstract: The modeling of bio-molecular system across molecular scales remains a central challenge in scientific research. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to bio-molecular discovery, yet systematic evaluation across multi-scale biological problems and rigorous assessment of their tool-augmented capabilities remain limited. We reveal a systematic gap between LLM performance and mechanistic understanding through the proposed cross-scale bio-molecular benchmark: BioMol-LLM-Bench, a unified framework comprising 26 downstream tasks that covers 4 distinct difficulty levels, and computational tools are integrated for a more comprehensive evaluation. Evaluation on 13 representative models reveals 4 main findings: chain-of-thought data provides limited benefit and may even reduce performance on biological tasks; hybrid mamba-attention architectures are more effective for long bio-molecular sequences; supervised fine-tuning improves specialization at the cost of generalization; and current LLMs perform well on classification tasks but remain weak on challenging regression tasks. Together, these findings provide practical guidance for future LLM-based modeling of molecular systems.

</details>


### [169] [Scalable Variational Bayesian Fine-Tuning of LLMs via Orthogonalized Low-Rank Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03388)
*Haotian Xiang,Bingcong Li,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PoLAR-VBLL：一种用于大语言模型贝叶斯微调的可扩展框架，通过正交化低秩适配器和变分贝叶斯推理实现更好的不确定性量化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键应用中部署大语言模型时，不确定性量化至关重要，但现有方法存在校准效果依赖训练轨迹或推理时计算开销大的问题，特别是在参数高效微调后模型容易过度自信

Method: 提出PoLAR-VBLL框架：1) PoLAR（极分解低秩适配器表示）使用正交化参数化和黎曼优化实现更稳定和表达力强的适配；2) 基于贝叶斯最后一层模型，在变分推理框架下联合优化PoLAR参数和最后一层参数的近似后验分布

Result: 实验验证了PoLAR-VBLL在多种常识推理任务上的有效性，在分布内和分布外数据上均表现出良好的泛化能力和不确定性估计性能

Conclusion: PoLAR-VBLL是一个灵活的框架，将架构增强优化与可扩展贝叶斯推理相结合，为大语言模型提供了良好校准的不确定性量化能力

Abstract: When deploying large language models (LLMs) to safety-critical applications, uncertainty quantification (UQ) is of utmost importance to self-assess the reliability of the LLM-based decisions. However, such decisions typically suffer from overconfidence, particularly after parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for downstream domain-specific tasks with limited data. Existing methods to alleviate this issue either rely on Laplace approximation based post-hoc framework, which may yield suboptimal calibration depending on the training trajectory, or variational Bayesian training that requires multiple complete forward passes through the entire LLM backbone at inference time for Monte Carlo estimation, posing scalability challenges for deployment. To address these limitations, we build on the Bayesian last layer (BLL) model, where the LLM-based deterministic feature extractor is followed by random last layer parameters for uncertainty reasoning. Since existing low-rank adapters (LoRA) for PEFT have limited expressiveness due to rank collapse, we address this with Polar-decomposed Low-rank Adapter Representation (PoLAR), an orthogonalized parameterization paired with Riemannian optimization to enable more stable and expressive adaptation. Building on this PoLAR-BLL model, we leverage the variational (V) inference framework to put forth a scalable Bayesian fine-tuning approach which jointly seeks the PoLAR parameters and approximate posterior of the last layer parameters via alternating optimization. The resulting PoLAR-VBLL is a flexible framework that nicely integrates architecture-enhanced optimization with scalable Bayesian inference to endow LLMs with well-calibrated UQ. Our empirical results verify the effectiveness of PoLAR-VBLL in terms of generalization and uncertainty estimation on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data for various common-sense reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [170] [MetaSAEs: Joint Training with a Decomposability Penalty Produces More Atomic Sparse Autoencoder Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03436)
*Matthew Levinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种联合训练目标来减少稀疏自编码器（SAE）潜在表示中的子空间混合问题，通过训练小型元SAE来稀疏重构主SAE的解码器列，从而提升特征的原子性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAEs）在安全相关应用（如对齐检测和模型引导）中日益重要，这些应用要求SAE潜在表示尽可能原子化，即每个潜在特征应代表从单一底层表示子空间提取的单一连贯概念。然而，实际中SAE潜在特征会将不同的表示子空间混合在一起，导致单个特征在语义不同的上下文中激活，模糊了模型计算的清晰图景。

Method: 引入联合训练目标直接惩罚子空间混合：训练一个小型元SAE与主SAE并行，元SAE稀疏重构主SAE的解码器列；当主SAE的解码器方向容易被元字典重构时，主SAE会受到惩罚。这发生在潜在方向位于其他主方向张成的子空间时。该方法通过梯度压力促使解码器方向更加相互独立，抵抗稀疏元压缩。

Result: 在GPT-2 large（第20层）上，所选配置使平均|φ|相对于相同数据训练的相同单独SAE降低了7.5%。自动可解释性（模糊测试）分数提高了7.6%，独立于训练和共现指标验证了原子性增益。重构开销适中。在Gemma 2 9B上的结果是方向性的：在未完全收敛的SAE上，相同参数化获得了最佳结果，ΔFuzz为+8.6%。定性分析确认，在多义词标记上激活的特征被拆分为语义不同的子特征，每个专门处理不同的表示子空间。

Conclusion: 提出的联合训练方法有效减少了SAE潜在表示中的子空间混合，提高了特征的原子性，在GPT-2 large上验证了性能提升，并在Gemma 2 9B上显示出有希望的迁移效果。该方法为提升SAE在安全关键应用中的实用性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly used for safety-relevant applications including alignment detection and model steering. These use cases require SAE latents to be as atomic as possible. Each latent should represent a single coherent concept drawn from a single underlying representational subspace. In practice, SAE latents blend representational subspaces together. A single feature can activate across semantically distinct contexts that share no true common representation, muddying an already complex picture of model computation. We introduce a joint training objective that directly penalizes this subspace blending. A small meta SAE is trained alongside the primary SAE to sparsely reconstruct the primary SAE's decoder columns; the primary SAE is penalized whenever its decoder directions are easy to reconstruct from the meta dictionary. This occurs whenever latent directions lie in a subspace spanned by other primary directions. This creates gradient pressure toward more mutually independent decoder directions that resist sparse meta-compression.
  On GPT-2 large (layer 20), the selected configuration reduces mean $|\varphi|$ by 7.5% relative to an identical solo SAE trained on the same data. Automated interpretability (fuzzing) scores improve by 7.6%, providing external validation of the atomicity gain independent of the training and co-occurrence metrics. Reconstruction overhead is modest. Results on Gemma 2 9B are directional. On not-fully-converged SAEs, the same parameterization yields the best results, a $+8.6\%$ $Δ$Fuzz. Though directional, this is an encouraging sign that the method transfers to a larger model. Qualitative analysis confirms that features firing on polysemantic tokens are split into semantically distinct sub-features, each specializing in a distinct representational subspace.

</details>


### [171] [Olmo Hybrid: From Theory to Practice and Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03444)
*William Merrill,Yanhong Li,Tyler Romero,Anej Svete,Caia Costello,Pradeep Dasigi,Dirk Groeneveld,David Heineman,Bailey Kuehl,Nathan Lambert,Jacob Morrison,Luca Soldaini,Finbarr Timbers,Pete Walsh,Noah A. Smith,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Ashish Sabharwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 混合模型（注意力+循环层）相比纯Transformer在表达能力、扩展效率和下游任务性能上均有优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非Transformer语言模型的潜力，特别是混合注意力与循环层的架构，验证其是否值得大规模扩展

Method: 理论分析混合模型表达能力，实践上训练Olmo Hybrid（7B参数），用Gated DeltaNet层替换滑动窗口层，与Olmo 3 7B对比评估

Result: 混合模型在预训练和中期评估中均优于纯Transformer，扩展效率更高，理论解释其表达能力优势可转化为更好的扩展效率

Conclusion: 混合注意力与循环层的模型是语言建模范式的有力扩展，不仅能减少推理内存，更是获得更具表达能力、扩展更高效的模型的基本方法

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated the potential of non-transformer language models, especially linear recurrent neural networks (RNNs) and hybrid models that mix recurrence and attention. Yet there is no consensus on whether the potential benefits of these new architectures justify the risk and effort of scaling them up. To address this, we provide evidence for the advantages of hybrid models over pure transformers on several fronts. First, theoretically, we show that hybrid models do not merely inherit the expressivity of transformers and linear RNNs, but can express tasks beyond both, such as code execution. Putting this theory to practice, we train Olmo Hybrid, a 7B-parameter model largely comparable to Olmo 3 7B but with the sliding window layers replaced by Gated DeltaNet layers. We show that Olmo Hybrid outperforms Olmo 3 across standard pretraining and mid-training evaluations, demonstrating the benefit of hybrid models in a controlled, large-scale setting. We find that the hybrid model scales significantly more efficiently than the transformer, explaining its higher performance. However, its unclear why greater expressivity on specific formal problems should result in better scaling or superior performance on downstream tasks unrelated to those problems. To explain this apparent gap, we return to theory and argue why increased expressivity should translate to better scaling efficiency, completing the loop. Overall, our results suggest that hybrid models mixing attention and recurrent layers are a powerful extension to the language modeling paradigm: not merely to reduce memory during inference, but as a fundamental way to obtain more expressive models that scale better during pretraining.

</details>


### [172] [Earth Embeddings Reveal Diverse Urban Signals from Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03456)
*Wenjing Gong,Udbhav Srivastava,Yuchen Wang,Yuhao Jia,Qifan Wu,Weishan Bai,Yifan Yang,Xiao Huang,Xinyue Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了三种地球嵌入模型（AlphaEarth、Prithvi、Clay）在城市邻里尺度监测中的性能，发现它们能有效捕捉城市变异，对与建成环境结构直接相关的指标预测效果最佳，但存在空间异质性和时间稳定性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统城市指标（来自普查、调查和行政记录）存在成本高、空间不一致、更新慢的问题。最近的地理空间基础模型能够生成可迁移的地球嵌入，但其在邻里尺度城市监测中的实用性尚不明确，需要系统评估。

Method: 采用统一的监督学习框架，在2020-2023年间对美国六个大都市区进行基准测试。使用三种地球嵌入模型（AlphaEarth、Prithvi、Clay）预测14个邻里级指标（涵盖犯罪、收入、健康、出行行为），并在四种设置下评估性能：全局、城市级、年度、城市-年度组合。

Result: 地球嵌入能捕捉显著的城市变异，对与建成环境结构直接相关的指标（如慢性健康负担、主要通勤模式）预测能力最强。与精细行为和地方政策相关的指标（如骑行）仍难以推断。预测性能在城市间差异显著但跨年相对稳定，显示空间异质性和时间稳健性。紧凑的64维AlphaEarth嵌入比降维后的Prithvi和Clay更有效。

Conclusion: 该研究为评估城市遥感中的地球嵌入建立了基准，证明了它们作为可扩展、低成本特征用于SDG对齐的邻里尺度城市监测的潜力，特别是对于建成环境相关的指标。

Abstract: Conventional urban indicators derived from censuses, surveys, and administrative records are often costly, spatially inconsistent, and slow to update. Recent geospatial foundation models enable Earth embeddings, compact satellite image representations transferable across downstream tasks, but their utility for neighborhood-scale urban monitoring remains unclear. Here, we benchmark three Earth embedding families, AlphaEarth, Prithvi, and Clay, for urban signal prediction across six U.S. metropolitan areas from 2020 to 2023. Using a unified supervised-learning framework, we predict 14 neighborhood-level indicators spanning crime, income, health, and travel behavior, and evaluate performance under four settings: global, city-wise, year-wise, and city-year. Results show that Earth embeddings capture substantial urban variation, with the highest predictive skill for outcomes more directly tied to built-environment structure, including chronic health burdens and dominant commuting modes. By contrast, indicators shaped more strongly by fine-scale behavior and local policy, such as cycling, remain difficult to infer. Predictive performance varies markedly across cities but remains comparatively stable across years, indicating strong spatial heterogeneity alongside temporal robustness. Exploratory analysis suggests that cross-city variation in predictive performance is associated with urban form in task-specific ways. Controlled dimensionality experiments show that representation efficiency is critical: compact 64-dimensional AlphaEarth embeddings remain more informative than 64-dimensional reductions of Prithvi and Clay. This study establishes a benchmark for evaluating Earth embeddings in urban remote sensing and demonstrates their potential as scalable, low-cost features for SDG-aligned neighborhood-scale urban monitoring.

</details>


### [173] [Super Agents and Confounders: Influence of surrounding agents on vehicle trajectory prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03463)
*Daniel Jost,Luca Paparusso,Martin Stoll,Jörg Wagner,Raghu Rajan,Joschka Bödecker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现当前轨迹预测模型存在关键缺陷：过多周围交通参与者信息反而降低预测精度，模型学习到不稳定、非因果的决策模式。提出条件信息瓶颈方法，无需额外监督即可有效压缩有用信息并忽略有害信息，提升预测性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度交互的驾驶场景中，轨迹预测通常依赖于周围交通参与者（车辆、行人）的信息。研究发现现有先进轨迹预测模型存在一个令人惊讶的关键缺陷：许多周围智能体信息反而会降低预测准确性而非改善。模型学习到不稳定且非因果的决策模式，在不同训练运行中变化显著。

Method: 使用基于Shapley值的归因分析揭示模型决策缺陷，提出集成条件信息瓶颈（CIB）方法。该方法无需额外监督，训练模型有效压缩智能体特征，同时忽略对预测任务无益的特征。通过多个数据集和模型架构进行综合实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，这种简单而有效的方法在许多情况下不仅提高了整体轨迹预测性能，还增强了对不同扰动的鲁棒性。条件信息瓶颈能够选择性地整合上下文信息，避免虚假或误导性信号的影响。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在轨迹预测中选择性整合上下文信息的重要性，因为这类信息常包含虚假或误导性信号。提供了识别非鲁棒行为的可解释性指标，并提出了一个有前景的解决方案方向。

Abstract: In highly interactive driving scenes, trajectory prediction is conditioned on information from surrounding traffic participants such as cars and pedestrians. Our main contribution is a comprehensive analysis of state-of-the-art trajectory predictors, which reveals a surprising and critical flaw: many surrounding agents degrade prediction accuracy rather than improve it. Using Shapley-based attribution, we rigorously demonstrate that models learn unstable and non-causal decision-making schemes that vary significantly across training runs. Building on these insights, we propose to integrate a Conditional Information Bottleneck (CIB), which does not require additional supervision and is trained to effectively compress agent features as well as ignore those that are not beneficial for the prediction task. Comprehensive experiments using multiple datasets and model architectures demonstrate that this simple yet effective approach not only improves overall trajectory prediction performance in many cases but also increases robustness to different perturbations. Our results highlight the importance of selectively integrating contextual information, which can often contain spurious or misleading signals, in trajectory prediction. Moreover, we provide interpretable metrics for identifying non-robust behavior and present a promising avenue towards a solution.

</details>


### [174] [Investigating Data Interventions for Subgroup Fairness: An ICU Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03478)
*Erin Tan,Judy Hanwen Shen,Irene Y. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 数据扩展对医疗AI模型子群性能的影响具有不确定性，传统"更好数据"的教条受到挑战，需要结合数据策略和模型校准来改善公平性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险医疗决策中，机器学习模型的算法偏见会加剧对某些子群的系统性伤害。偏见通常源于训练数据，而实践中"修复数据"的干预措施依赖于可用的额外数据源，但这些数据源往往不理想。数据扩展对子群性能的影响变得不稳定，因为样本量增加带来的改善被训练集分布偏移所抵消。

Method: 研究在医疗背景下结合数据源改善子群性能的局限性。使用两个临床数据集：eICU协作研究数据库和MIMIC-IV数据集。比较基于模型的后期校准方法和以数据为中心的添加策略，探索两者结合的重要性。评估数据添加对模型公平性和性能的影响，测试直观数据选择策略的可靠性。

Result: 数据添加既可能帮助也可能损害模型的公平性和性能，许多直观的数据选择策略不可靠。研究发现结合基于模型的后期校准和以数据为中心的添加策略对于改善子群性能都很重要。传统"更好数据"的教条受到质疑。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖"更好数据"无法解决公平性挑战，需要同时采用数据和模型方法。在医疗AI应用中，数据扩展对子群性能的影响具有不确定性，必须谨慎评估数据添加策略，并结合模型校准技术来确保公平性和性能的同步提升。

Abstract: In high-stakes settings where machine learning models are used to automate decision-making about individuals, the presence of algorithmic bias can exacerbate systemic harm to certain subgroups of people. These biases often stem from the underlying training data. In practice, interventions to "fix the data" depend on the actual additional data sources available -- where many are less than ideal. In these cases, the effects of data scaling on subgroup performance become volatile, as the improvements from increased sample size are counteracted by the introduction of distribution shifts in the training set. In this paper, we investigate the limitations of combining data sources to improve subgroup performance within the context of healthcare. Clinical models are commonly trained on datasets comprised of patient electronic health record (EHR) data from different hospitals or admission departments. Across two such datasets, the eICU Collaborative Research Database and the MIMIC-IV dataset, we find that data addition can both help and hurt model fairness and performance, and many intuitive strategies for data selection are unreliable. We compare model-based post-hoc calibration and data-centric addition strategies to find that the combination of both is important to improve subgroup performance. Our work questions the traditional dogma of "better data" for overcoming fairness challenges by comparing and combining data- and model-based approaches.

</details>


### [175] [Online learning of smooth functions on $\mathbb{R}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03525)
*Jesse Geneson,Kuldeep Singh,Alexander Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究实数域上实值函数的对抗性在线学习，发现标准模型在ℝ上不适定，提出三种改进场景限制远距离查询的影响，分析各场景的尖锐特征，并扩展到多维情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究实数域ℝ上实值函数的对抗性在线学习问题，发现对于绝对连续函数类𝒢_q（导数L^q范数有界），标准模型在ℝ上不适定：对于任意p≥1和q>1，对手可以迫使无限损失。这促使研究者设计改进的学习场景来限制远离已观测输入的查询影响。

Method: 提出三种改进的学习场景：场景1要求对手的新查询必须在过去某个查询的1距离内；场景2允许对手任意查询，但学习者只对查询在过去查询1距离内的轮次受罚；场景3在轮次t的损失乘以权重g(min_{j<t}|x_t-x_j|)。分析这些场景在不同参数下的尖锐特征，并扩展到多维切片类𝒢_{q,d}。

Result: 1. 标准模型在ℝ上不适定：对任意p≥1和q>1，对手可迫使无限损失；2. 场景1-2在多个参数范围内获得尖锐特征；3. 场景3存在阈值现象：若g衰减过慢，对手可迫使无限加权损失；若g快速衰减（如g(z)=e^{-cz}），在p=q=2时获得有限且尖锐的保证；4. 多维扩展显示尖锐二分：一维情况在某些参数范围内有有限最优值，但对任意d≥2，切片类𝒢_{q,d}过于宽松，即使在场景1-3下对手也可迫使无限损失。

Conclusion: 实数域上的对抗性在线学习需要限制查询空间或加权损失才能获得有限保证。三种改进场景提供了不同的限制方式，场景3的权重衰减速率是关键因素。多维情况比一维更具挑战性，二维及以上维度即使采用改进场景也无法获得有限保证，表明高维函数类的复杂性显著增加。

Abstract: We study adversarial online learning of real-valued functions on $\mathbb{R}$. In each round the learner is queried at $x_t\in\mathbb{R}$, predicts $\hat y_t$, and then observes the true value $f(x_t)$; performance is measured by cumulative $p$-loss $\sum_{t\ge 1}|\hat y_t-f(x_t)|^p$. For the class \[ \mathcal{G}_q=\Bigl\{f:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}\ \text{absolutely continuous}:\ \int_{\mathbb{R}}|f'(x)|^q\,dx\le 1\Bigr\}, \] we show that the standard model becomes ill-posed on $\mathbb{R}$: for every $p\ge 1$ and $q>1$, an adversary can force infinite loss. Motivated by this obstruction, we analyze three modified learning scenarios that limit the influence of queries that are far from previously observed inputs. In Scenario 1 the adversary must choose each new query within distance $1$ of some past query. In Scenario 2 the adversary may query anywhere, but the learner is penalized only on rounds whose query lies within distance $1$ of a past query. In Scenario 3 the loss in round $t$ is multiplied by a weight $g(\min_{j<t}|x_t-x_j|)$.
  We obtain sharp characterizations for Scenarios 1-2 in several regimes. For Scenario 3 we identify a clean threshold phenomenon: if $g$ decays too slowly, then the adversary can force infinite weighted loss. In contrast, for rapidly decaying weights such as $g(z)=e^{-cz}$ we obtain finite and sharp guarantees in the quadratic case $p=q=2$. Finally, we study a natural multivariable slice generalization $\mathcal{G}_{q,d}$ of $\mathcal{G}_q$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$ and show a sharp dichotomy: while the one-dimensional case admits finite opt-values in certain regimes, for every $d\ge 2$ the slice class $\mathcal{G}_{q,d}$ is too permissive, and even under Scenarios 1-3 an adversary can force infinite loss.

</details>


### [176] [Choosing the Right Regularizer for Applied ML: Simulation Benchmarks of Popular Scikit-learn Regularization Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03541)
*Benjamin S. Knight,Ahsaas Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统回顾了正则化方法的历史发展，并通过大规模模拟实验比较了Ridge、Lasso、ElasticNet和Post-Lasso OLS四种经典框架的性能，发现在样本特征比足够时预测精度相近，但Lasso在多共线性下召回率严重下降，为此提供了基于可观测特征属性的决策指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化方法在机器学习中广泛应用，但不同方法在不同场景下的性能差异缺乏系统性评估。研究者旨在通过历史回顾和大规模模拟实验，为实践者提供基于实证证据的正则化方法选择指导。

Method: 采用历史文献综述与大规模模拟实验相结合的方法。首先系统梳理正则化从1960年代逐步回归到现代方法的发展历程，然后在基于8个生产级机器学习模型的7维参数空间上进行134,400次模拟，评估Ridge、Lasso、ElasticNet和Post-Lasso OLS四种框架的性能。

Result: 当样本特征比足够大（n/p ≥ 78）时，Ridge、Lasso和ElasticNet的预测精度几乎相同。但在高条件数（kappa）和低信噪比（SNR）的多共线性场景下，Lasso的召回率从0.93急剧下降至0.18，而ElasticNet保持稳定。Post-Lasso OLS在相同条件下也表现不佳。

Conclusion: 研究建议实践者在高条件数和小样本量场景下避免使用Lasso或Post-Lasso OLS，推荐使用ElasticNet。研究最终提供了基于可观测特征空间属性的客观决策指南，帮助机器学习工程师在scikit-learn支持的框架中选择最优方法。

Abstract: This study surveys the historical development of regularization, tracing its evolution from stepwise regression in the 1960s to recent advancements in formal error control, structured penalties for non-independent features, Bayesian methods, and l0-based regularization (among other techniques). We empirically evaluate the performance of four canonical frameworks -- Ridge, Lasso, ElasticNet, and Post-Lasso OLS -- across 134,400 simulations spanning a 7-dimensional manifold grounded in eight production-grade machine learning models. Our findings demonstrate that for prediction accuracy when the sample-to-feature ratio is sufficient (n/p >= 78), Ridge, Lasso, and ElasticNet are nearly interchangeable. However, we find that Lasso recall is highly fragile under multicollinearity; at high condition numbers (kappa) and low SNR, Lasso recall collapses to 0.18 while ElasticNet maintains 0.93. Consequently, we advise practitioners against using Lasso or Post-Lasso OLS at high kappa with small sample sizes. The analysis concludes with an objective-driven decision guide to assist machine learning engineers in selecting the optimal scikit-learn-supported framework based on observable feature space attributes.

</details>


### [177] [BlazeFL: Fast and Deterministic Federated Learning Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03606)
*Kitsuya Azuma,Takayuki Nishio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BlazeFL是一个轻量级联邦学习仿真框架，通过共享内存执行和确定性随机数管理，解决了并行客户端训练中的非确定性问题，在保持结果可重现的同时显著提升执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究中广泛使用单节点仿真，但并行客户端训练常因共享随机状态和调度可变性引入非确定性，迫使研究者在吞吐量和可重现性之间做出权衡，或需在复杂框架中实现自定义控制逻辑。

Method: BlazeFL采用线程级并行和内存中参数交换，避免序列化和进程间通信开销；为支持确定性执行，为每个客户端分配隔离的随机数生成器流；在固定软硬件栈下，当随机操作使用BlazeFL管理的生成器时，可确保多次高并发运行的比特级一致性结果。

Result: 在CIFAR-10图像分类实验中，BlazeFL相对于广泛使用的开源基线显著减少执行时间，在通信密集型工作负载上实现高达3.1倍的加速，同时保持轻量级依赖占用。

Conclusion: BlazeFL通过共享内存执行和确定性随机数管理，有效缓解了联邦学习仿真中吞吐量与可重现性之间的权衡，为研究社区提供了一个高效、可重现的轻量级仿真框架。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research increasingly relies on single-node simulations with hundreds or thousands of virtual clients, making both efficiency and reproducibility essential. Yet parallel client training often introduces nondeterminism through shared random state and scheduling variability, forcing researchers to trade throughput for reproducibility or to implement custom control logic within complex frameworks. We present BlazeFL, a lightweight framework for single-node FL simulation that alleviates this trade-off through free-threaded shared-memory execution and deterministic randomness management. BlazeFL uses thread-based parallelism with in-memory parameter exchange between the server and clients, avoiding serialization and inter-process communication overhead. To support deterministic execution, BlazeFL assigns isolated random number generator (RNG) streams to clients. Under a fixed software/hardware stack, and when stochastic operators consume BlazeFL-managed generators, this design yields bitwise-identical results across repeated high-concurrency runs in both thread-based and process-based modes. In CIFAR-10 image-classification experiments, BlazeFL substantially reduces execution time relative to a widely used open-source baseline, achieving up to 3.1$\times$ speedup on communication-dominated workloads while preserving a lightweight dependency footprint. Our open-source implementation is available at: https://github.com/kitsuyaazuma/blazefl.

</details>


### [178] [Algebraic Diversity: Group-Theoretic Spectral Estimation from Single Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03634)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明：对于二阶统计估计，多观测的时间平均可被单观测的代数群作用替代。通用替换定理确立了群平均估计器与多快照协方差估计具有等价子空间分解的条件，最优性定理证明对称群是普遍最优的（产生KL变换）。该框架将DFT、DCT和KLT统一为群匹配谱变换的特例，并通过闭式双交换子特征值问题实现多项式时间最优群选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二阶统计估计通常需要多个时间快照来构建协方差矩阵，这在实际应用中存在局限性。本文旨在探索是否可以通过代数群作用在单次观测上实现等效的统计估计，从而克服多快照需求带来的限制。

Method: 提出通用替换定理，建立群平均估计器与多快照协方差估计的等价性条件。证明最优性定理，表明对称群能产生最优KL变换。开发闭式双交换子特征值问题框架，用于多项式时间最优群选择。将DFT、DCT、KLT统一为群匹配谱变换的特例。

Result: 1. 单快照MUSIC DOA估计；2. 大规模MIMO信道估计实现64%吞吐量增益；3. 单脉冲波形分类达到90%准确率；4. 非阿贝尔群的图信号处理；5. 对transformer LLMs的代数分析发现：RoPE在五个模型（22,480个头观测）中70-80%的注意力头使用了错误的代数群，最优群是内容依赖的，基于谱集中的剪枝在13B规模上改善了困惑度。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了二阶统计估计中时间平均与代数群作用的等价性，为信号处理、通信和机器学习提供了统一的数学基础。特别在transformer架构分析中揭示了RoPE机制的根本缺陷，并提出了改进方案。所有诊断仅需单次前向传播，无需梯度计算或训练。

Abstract: We prove that temporal averaging over multiple observations can be replaced by algebraic group action on a single observation for second-order statistical estimation. A General Replacement Theorem establishes conditions under which a group-averaged estimator from one snapshot achieves equivalent subspace decomposition to multi-snapshot covariance estimation, and an Optimality Theorem proves that the symmetric group is universally optimal (yielding the KL transform). The framework unifies the DFT, DCT, and KLT as special cases of group-matched spectral transforms, with a closed-form double-commutator eigenvalue problem for polynomial-time optimal group selection. Five applications are demonstrated: MUSIC DOA estimation from a single snapshot, massive MIMO channel estimation with 64% throughput gain, single-pulse waveform classification at 90% accuracy, graph signal processing with non-Abelian groups, and a new algebraic analysis of transformer LLMs revealing that RoPE uses the wrong algebraic group for 70-80% of attention heads across five models (22,480 head observations), that the optimal group is content-dependent, and that spectral-concentration-based pruning improves perplexity at the 13B scale. All diagnostics require a single forward pass with no gradients or training.

</details>


### [179] [Delayed Homomorphic Reinforcement Learning for Environments with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03641)
*Jongsoo Lee,Jangwon Kim,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出延迟同态强化学习（DHRL）框架，通过MDP同态理论压缩信念等价增强状态，解决延迟反馈导致的马尔可夫性破坏问题，避免状态空间爆炸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界强化学习常面临延迟反馈问题，破坏马尔可夫假设并阻碍学习与控制。传统状态增强方法导致状态空间爆炸，带来严重的样本复杂度负担。现有增强基线方法不完整，要么主要减轻critic负担，要么对actor和critic采用非统一处理。

Method: 提出延迟同态强化学习（DHRL）框架，基于MDP同态理论，通过压缩信念等价增强状态，在抽象MDP上进行高效策略学习而不损失最优性。提供理论分析包括状态空间压缩边界和样本复杂度，并引入实用算法。

Result: 在MuJoCo基准的连续控制任务实验中，该算法优于强大的增强基线方法，特别是在长延迟条件下表现更佳。

Conclusion: DHRL框架为延迟反馈强化学习提供了结构化且样本高效的解决方案，通过MDP同态理论有效处理状态空间爆炸问题，在保持最优性的同时显著提升学习效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in real-world systems is often accompanied by delayed feedback, which breaks the Markov assumption and impedes both learning and control. Canonical state augmentation approaches cause the state-space explosion, which introduces a severe sample-complexity burden. Despite recent progress, the state-of-the-art augmentation-based baselines remain incomplete: they either predominantly reduce the burden on the critic or adopt non-unified treatments for the actor and critic. To provide a structured and sample-efficient solution, we propose delayed homomorphic reinforcement learning (DHRL), a framework grounded in MDP homomorphisms that collapses belief-equivalent augmented states and enables efficient policy learning on the resulting abstract MDP without loss of optimality. We provide theoretical analyses of state-space compression bounds and sample complexity, and introduce a practical algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks in MuJoCo benchmark confirm that our algorithm outperforms strong augmentation-based baselines, particularly under long delays.

</details>


### [180] [Automated Attention Pattern Discovery at Scale in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03764)
*Jonathan Katzy,Razvan-Mihai Popescu,Erik Mekkes,Arie van Deursen,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AP-MAE方法，通过视觉Transformer重建注意力模式，实现大语言模型的可扩展可解释性分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释性方法难以扩展到大型语言模型，需要一种能够分析重复行为且可扩展的方法

Method: 从Java代码数据集中挖掘完成场景，收集注意力模式，提出基于视觉Transformer的AP-MAE模型来重建掩码注意力模式

Result: AP-MAE能高精度重建注意力模式，跨模型泛化，发现重复模式，预测生成正确性（55%-70%准确率），选择性干预提升13.6%准确率

Conclusion: 注意力模式是可扩展的可解释性信号，AP-MAE为大规模可解释性提供了可转移的分析和干预基础

Abstract: Large language models have found success by scaling up capabilities to work in general settings. The same can unfortunately not be said for interpretability methods. The current trend in mechanistic interpretability is to provide precise explanations of specific behaviors in controlled settings. These often do not generalize, or are too resource intensive for larger studies. In this work we propose to study repeated behaviors in large language models by mining completion scenarios in Java code datasets, through exploiting the structured nature of code. We collect the attention patterns generated in the attention heads to demonstrate that they are scalable signals for global interpretability of model components. We show that vision models offer a promising direction for analyzing attention patterns at scale. To demonstrate this, we introduce the Attention Pattern - Masked Autoencoder(AP-MAE), a vision transformer-based model that efficiently reconstructs masked attention patterns. Experiments on StarCoder2 show that AP-MAE (i) reconstructs masked attention patterns with high accuracy, (ii) generalizes across unseen models with minimal degradation, (iii) reveals recurring patterns across inferences, (iv) predicts whether a generation will be correct without access to ground truth, with accuracies ranging from 55% to 70% depending on the task, and (v) enables targeted interventions that increase accuracy by 13.6% when applied selectively, but cause collapse when applied excessively. These results establish attention patterns as a scalable signal for interpretability and demonstrate that AP-MAE provides a transferable foundation for both analysis and intervention in large language models. Beyond its standalone value, AP-MAE also serves as a selection procedure to guide fine-grained mechanistic approaches. We release code and models to support future work in large-scale interpretability.

</details>


### [181] [CountsDiff: A Diffusion Model on the Natural Numbers for Generation and Imputation of Count-Based Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03779)
*Renzo G. Soatto,Anders Hoel,Greycen Ren,Shorna Alam,Stephen Bates,Nikolaos P. Daskalakis,Caroline Uhler,Maria Skoularidou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CountsDiff是一个专门为自然数分布设计的扩散框架，通过重新参数化Blackout扩散框架，引入连续时间训练、无分类器引导等现代扩散模型特性，在图像生成和单细胞RNA-seq插补任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在连续和基于token的领域表现出色，但在离散序数数据（如自然数）上的应用仍然不足。特别是生物学中的计数数据（如单细胞RNA-seq）需要专门的建模方法。

Method: CountsDiff扩展了Blackout扩散框架，通过生存概率调度和显式损失加权的直接参数化简化了公式。引入了连续时间训练、无分类器引导、churn/remasking反向动力学等现代扩散模型特性，允许非单调的反向轨迹。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CelebA等自然图像数据集上验证了框架的有效性。在单细胞RNA-seq插补任务中，即使简单的实现也能匹配或超越最先进的离散生成模型和领先的RNA-seq插补方法，为未来优化设计留下了巨大提升空间。

Conclusion: CountsDiff为自然数分布提供了一个灵活且有效的扩散建模框架，在图像生成和生物计数数据建模方面都表现出强大潜力，特别是在单细胞RNA-seq插补任务中展现了卓越性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models have excelled at generative tasks for both continuous and token-based domains, but their application to discrete ordinal data remains underdeveloped. We present CountsDiff, a diffusion framework designed to natively model distributions on the natural numbers. CountsDiff extends the Blackout diffusion framework by simplifying its formulation through a direct parameterization in terms of a survival probability schedule and an explicit loss weighting. This introduces flexibility through design parameters with direct analogues in existing diffusion modeling frameworks. Beyond this reparameterization, CountsDiff introduces features from modern diffusion models, previously absent in counts-based domains, including continuous-time training, classifier-free guidance, and churn/remasking reverse dynamics that allow non-monotone reverse trajectories. We propose an initial instantiation of CountsDiff and validate it on natural image datasets (CIFAR-10, CelebA), exploring the effects of varying the introduced design parameters in a complex, well-studied, and interpretable data domain. We then highlight biological count assays as a natural use case, evaluating CountsDiff on single-cell RNA-seq imputation in a fetal cell and heart cell atlas. Remarkably, we find that even this simple instantiation matches or surpasses the performance of a state-of-the-art discrete generative model and leading RNA-seq imputation methods, while leaving substantial headroom for further gains through optimized design choices in future work.

</details>


### [182] [k-Maximum Inner Product Attention for Graph Transformers and the Expressive Power of GraphGPS The Expressive Power of GraphGPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03815)
*Jonas De Schouwer,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Xiaowen Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出k-MIP注意力机制，通过top-k选择最相关节点实现线性复杂度，在保持表达能力的同时显著提升图transformer的可扩展性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图transformer的全连接注意力机制具有二次复杂度，难以扩展到大规模图；现有线性化或受限注意力方法往往牺牲性能或表达能力

Method: 引入k-MIP注意力机制，基于top-k操作为每个查询选择最相关的关键节点，结合符号矩阵计算注意力分数，实现线性内存复杂度和实际加速

Result: 在单块A100 GPU上可处理超过50万节点的图，相比全连接注意力实现高达一个数量级的加速；在Long Range Graph Benchmark、City-Networks基准和自定义大规模点云数据集上表现优异

Conclusion: k-MIP注意力机制在保持图transformer表达能力的同时显著提升了可扩展性，为大规模图处理提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Graph transformers have shown promise in overcoming limitations of traditional graph neural networks, such as oversquashing and difficulties in modelling long-range dependencies. However, their application to large-scale graphs is hindered by the quadratic memory and computational complexity of the all-to-all attention mechanism. Although alternatives such as linearized attention and restricted attention patterns have been proposed, these often degrade performance or limit expressive power. To better balance efficiency and effectiveness, we introduce k-Maximum Inner Product (k-MIP) attention for graph transformers. k-MIP attention selects the most relevant key nodes per query via a top-k operation, yielding a sparse yet flexible attention pattern. Combined with an attention score computation based on symbolic matrices, this results in linear memory complexity and practical speedups of up to an order of magnitude compared to all-to-all attention, enabling the processing of graphs with over 500k nodes on a single A100 GPU. We provide a theoretical analysis of expressive power, showing that k-MIP attention does not compromise the expressiveness of graph transformers: specifically, we prove that k-MIP transformers can approximate any full-attention transformer to arbitrary precision. In addition, we analyze the expressive power of the GraphGPS framework, in which we integrate our attention mechanism, and establish an upper bound on its graph distinguishing capability in terms of the S-SEG-WL test. Finally, we validate our approach on the Long Range Graph Benchmark, the City-Networks benchmark, and two custom large-scale inductive point cloud datasets, consistently ranking among the top-performing scalable graph transformers.

</details>


### [183] [Collapse-Free Prototype Readout Layer for Transformer Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03850)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione,Rahul Ranjeev Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DDCL-Attention是一种基于原型的transformer编码器读出层，通过学习的压缩机制替代简单池化方法，使用少量全局原型向量并通过软概率匹配分配token，以线性复杂度生成紧凑的token摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统transformer编码器使用简单池化方法（如均值池化或类别token）进行特征聚合存在局限性，需要更有效的压缩机制来生成紧凑且有意义的token表示。

Method: 使用少量全局原型向量，通过软概率匹配将token分配给原型，实现token压缩。方法包含三个关键设计：1）通过训练损失的精确分解（重构项+多样性项）避免原型崩溃；2）基于Tikhonov奇异摄动理论和显式学习率约束确保与编码器联合训练的稳定性；3）支持三种应用模式：最终读出层、可微分码本（扩展VQ-VAE）和分层文档压缩器。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验验证了理论预测：损失分解精确成立，满足稳定性条件时原型分离度按预期增长，码本达到完全利用率且优于标准硬向量量化。在轨道碎片分类任务上的额外研究表明该方法也适用于科学表格数据等非标准NLP和视觉任务。

Conclusion: DDCL-Attention提供了一种有效且稳定的原型基读出层，能够避免原型崩溃，确保训练稳定性，并在多种应用中表现优异，扩展了transformer架构在多样化任务中的应用能力。

Abstract: DDCL-Attention is a prototype-based readout layer for transformer encoders that replaces simple pooling methods, such as mean pooling or class tokens, with a learned compression mechanism. It uses a small set of global prototype vectors and assigns tokens to them through soft probabilistic matching, producing compact token summaries at linear complexity in sequence length.
  The method offers three main advantages. First, it avoids prototype collapse through an exact decomposition of the training loss into a reconstruction term and a diversity term, ensuring that prototypes remain distinct. Second, its joint training with the encoder is shown to be stable under a practical timescale condition, using Tikhonov's singular perturbation theory and explicit learning-rate constraints. Third, the same framework supports three uses: a final readout layer, a differentiable codebook extending VQ-VAE, and a hierarchical document compressor.
  Experiments on four datasets confirm the theoretical predictions: the loss decomposition holds exactly, prototype separation grows as expected when the stability condition is met, and the codebook reaches full utilization, outperforming standard hard vector quantization. An additional study on orbital debris classification shows that the method also applies beyond standard NLP and vision tasks, including scientific tabular data.

</details>


### [184] [Where to Steer: Input-Dependent Layer Selection for Steering Improves LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03867)
*Soham Gadgil,Chris Lin,Su-In Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: W2S框架通过输入自适应的层选择机制，改进了传统固定层干预的steering vector方法，在多个LLM和对齐任务中表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有steering vector方法通常在全局固定层应用干预，隐含假设最优干预层对所有输入是不变的。作者认为这一假设存在根本限制，因为与目标行为相关的表示可能在不同输入中编码于不同层。

Method: 提出W2S框架，通过学习从输入嵌入到最优steering层的映射，根据输入自适应选择干预层。该方法包含理论分析和实证验证，证明最优steering层在不同输入间存在显著变化。

Result: 在多个LLM和对齐行为上，W2S一致优于固定层基线方法，在分布内和分布外设置中均有改进。自适应层选择成为steering vector方法中缺失的关键设计维度。

Conclusion: 输入依赖的控制在LLM对齐中至关重要，自适应层选择是当前steering vector方法中缺失的关键设计维度。W2S框架通过条件化层选择机制，显著提升了steering vector方法的有效性。

Abstract: Steering vectors have emerged as a lightweight and effective approach for aligning large language models (LLMs) at inference time, enabling modulation over model behaviors by shifting LLM representations towards a target behavior. However, existing methods typically apply steering vectors at a globally fixed layer, implicitly assuming that the optimal intervention layer is invariant across inputs. We argue that this assumption is fundamentally limited, as representations relevant to a target behavior can be encoded at different layers depending on the input. Theoretically, we show that different inputs can require steering at different layers to achieve alignment with a desirable model behavior. We also provide empirical evidence that the optimal steering layer varies substantially across inputs in practice. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Where to Steer (W2S), a framework that adaptively selects the intervention layer conditioned on the input, by learning a mapping from input embeddings to optimal steering layers. Across multiple LLMs and alignment behaviors, W2S consistently outperforms fixed-layer baselines, with improvements in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Our findings highlight the importance of input-dependent control in LLM alignment and demonstrate that adaptive layer selection is a key design dimension missing in the current methodology of steering vectors.

</details>


### [185] [SODA: Semi On-Policy Black-Box Distillation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03873)
*Xiwen Chen,Jingjing Wang,Wenhui Zhu,Peijie Qiu,Xuanzhao Dong,Hejian Sang,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard,Feng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SODA提出了一种半在线知识蒸馏方法，通过对比教师最优响应与学生静态次优输出来实现高效分布对齐，避免了传统方法的训练不稳定性和高计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒知识蒸馏方法存在严格权衡：简单的离线方法无法纠正学生模型的固有错误，而完全在线方法虽然能解决此问题但引入训练不稳定性和巨大计算开销。前沿教师模型与小型基础模型之间存在固有能力差距，这为更高效的蒸馏方法提供了机会。

Method: SODA（半在线对齐蒸馏）利用教师模型的最优响应与学生模型一次性静态输出快照构建对比信号。由于小型学生模型的零样本响应几乎严格劣于强大教师的目标响应，这种静态对比足以实现高质量分布对齐，无需昂贵的动态rollout和脆弱的对抗平衡。

Result: 在四个紧凑型Qwen2.5和Llama-3模型上的广泛评估显示，SODA在16个基准测试中的15个上匹配或超越了最先进方法。更重要的是，它在实现优异蒸馏质量的同时，训练速度快10倍，峰值GPU内存消耗减少27%，并完全消除了对抗不稳定性。

Conclusion: SODA证明了向小型学生模型暴露其自身静态次优行为足以实现高质量分布对齐，无需昂贵的动态rollout和对抗平衡。这种半在线范式为大型语言模型的高效知识蒸馏提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Black-box knowledge distillation for large language models presents a strict trade-off. Simple off-policy methods (e.g., sequence-level knowledge distillation) struggle to correct the student's inherent errors. Fully on-policy methods (e.g., Generative Adversarial Distillation) solve this via adversarial training but introduce well-known training instability and crippling computational overhead. To address this dilemma, we propose SODA (Semi On-policy Distillation with Alignment), a highly efficient alternative motivated by the inherent capability gap between frontier teachers and much smaller base models. Because a compact student model's natural, zero-shot responses are almost strictly inferior to the powerful teacher's targets, we can construct a highly effective contrastive signal simply by pairing the teacher's optimal response with a one-time static snapshot of the student's outputs. This demonstrates that exposing the small student to its own static inferior behaviors is sufficient for high-quality distribution alignment, eliminating the need for costly dynamic rollouts and fragile adversarial balancing. Extensive evaluations across four compact Qwen2.5 and Llama-3 models validate this semi on-policy paradigm. SODA matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art methods on 15 out of 16 benchmark results. More importantly, it achieves this superior distillation quality while training 10 times faster, consuming 27% less peak GPU memory, and completely eliminating adversarial instability.

</details>


### [186] [Learning An Interpretable Risk Scoring System for Maximizing Decision Net Benefit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04241)
*Wenhao Chi,Ş. İlker Birbil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种直接优化净效益的风险评分系统，通过稀疏整数线性规划构建具有整数系数的透明评分系统，在保持良好区分度和校准性能的同时最大化净效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险评分系统通常关注预测准确性或基于似然的标准，但这些指标可能与最大化效用的主要目标不一致。需要开发直接优化净效益的方法，同时保持模型的解释性和实用性。

Method: 将风险评分系统构建为稀疏整数线性规划问题，生成具有整数系数的透明评分系统。该方法直接在一系列决策阈值上优化净效益，并建立了净效益、区分度和校准之间的基本关系。

Result: 在多个公共数据集和真实临床数据集上的评估表明，该方法能有效实现高净效益，同时保持有竞争力的区分度和校准性能。分析证明优化净效益也能保证传统性能指标。

Conclusion: 提出的方法提供了一种直接优化净效益的透明风险评分系统，具有整数系数便于解释和实际应用，在保持良好预测性能的同时更好地服务于决策目标。

Abstract: Risk scoring systems are widely used in high-stakes domains to assist decision-making. However, existing approaches often focus on optimizing predictive accuracy or likelihood-based criteria, which may not align with the main goal of maximizing utility. In this paper, we propose a novel risk scoring system that directly optimizes net benefit over a range of decision thresholds. The model is formulated as a sparse integer linear programming problem which enables the construction of a transparent scoring system with integer coefficients, and hence, facilitates interpretation and practical application. We also establish fundamental relationships among net benefit, discrimination, and calibration. Our analysis proves that optimizing net benefit also guarantees conventional performance measures. We thoroughly evaluated our method on multiple public datasets as well as on a real-world clinical dataset. This computational study demonstrated that our interpretable method can effectively achieve high net benefit while maintaining competitive discrimination and calibration performance.

</details>


### [187] [Spatiotemporal Interpolation of GEDI Biomass with Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03874)
*Robin Young,Srinivasan Keshav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩展注意力神经过程框架，利用地理空间基础模型嵌入，在稀疏时空设置下联合插值GEDI激光雷达生物量数据，解决时间间隙和扰动事件中的不确定性量化问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEDI激光雷达提供了可靠的森林地上生物量密度估计，但其轨道采样导致时空覆盖不规则，且存在长时间观测中断（如2023-2024年的13个月休眠期）。现有机器学习方法主要填补空间间隙，但时间插值特别是跨扰动事件的时间插值尚未解决，且标准集成方法产生系统性的错误校准预测区间。

Method: 扩展注意力神经过程框架，将空间和时间对称处理，利用地理空间基础模型嵌入，在稀疏时空设置下进行联合插值。通过空间换时间替代，利用其他时间邻近位置的观测来预测缺失时期的生物量。

Result: ANP方法在各类扰动机制下产生良好校准的不确定性估计，支持其在需要可靠不确定性量化的森林碳核算测量、报告与核查应用中的使用。

Conclusion: 扩展的注意力神经过程框架能够有效解决GEDI数据的时间间隙问题，提供跨扰动事件的可靠生物量时间插值和良好校准的不确定性量化，满足森林碳核算的MRV应用需求。

Abstract: Monitoring deforestation-driven carbon emissions requires both spatially explicit and temporally continuous estimates of aboveground biomass density (AGBD) with calibrated uncertainty. NASA's Global Ecosystem Dynamics Investigation (GEDI) provides reliable LIDAR-derived AGBD, but its orbital sampling causes irregular spatiotemporal coverage, and occasional operational interruptions, including a 13-month hibernation from March 2023 to April 2024, leave extended gaps in the observational record. Prior work has used machine learning approaches to fill GEDI's spatial gaps using satellite-derived features, but temporal interpolation of biomass through unobserved periods, particularly across active disturbance events, remains largely unaddressed. Moreover, standard ensemble methods for biomass mapping have been shown to produce systematically miscalibrated prediction intervals. To address these gaps, we extend the Attentive Neural Process (ANP) framework, previously applied to spatial biomass interpolation, to jointly sparse spatiotemporal settings using geospatial foundation model embeddings. We treat space and time symmetrically, empirically validating a form of space-for-time substitution in which observations from nearby locations at other times inform predictions at held-out periods. Our results demonstrate that the ANP produces well-calibrated uncertainty estimates across disturbance regimes, supporting its use in Measurement, Reporting, and Verification (MRV) applications that require reliable uncertainty quantification for forest carbon accounting.

</details>


### [188] [Regime-Calibrated Demand Priors for Ride-Hailing Fleet Dispatch and Repositioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03883)
*Indar Kumar,Akanksha Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于需求机制校准的网约车调度方法，通过历史数据分段、相似性匹配和校准需求先验，结合线性规划车队重新定位和匈牙利匹配批量调度，显著降低乘客等待时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车调度需要准确预测随时间、星期、季节和特殊事件变化的需求模式，现有方法难以有效处理这些高度变化的需求模式。

Method: 1) 将历史行程数据分段为需求机制；2) 通过包含Kolmogorov-Smirnov距离、Wasserstein-1距离、特征距离、方差比、事件模式相似性和时间邻近度的相似性集成，将当前运营时段与最相似的历史类似时段匹配；3) 使用校准后的需求先验驱动基于线性规划的车队重新定位策略和匈牙利匹配批量调度。

Result: 在520万纽约TLC行程数据的8个多样化场景中，平均等待时间降低31.1%，P95等待时间降低37.6%，等待时间基尼系数从0.441改善至0.409。校准贡献16.9%的改进，线性规划重新定位贡献15.5%。方法无需训练、可解释，在芝加哥验证中等待时间降低23.3%，对不同车队规模保持稳健。

Conclusion: 提出的机制校准方法通过有效匹配历史相似需求模式，结合优化调度策略，显著改善了网约车调度性能，具有无需训练、可解释、可泛化和稳健的优点。

Abstract: Effective ride-hailing dispatch requires anticipating demand patterns that vary substantially across time-of-day, day-of-week, season, and special events. We propose a regime-calibrated approach that (i) segments historical trip data into demand regimes, (ii) matches the current operating period to the most similar historical analogues via a similarity ensemble combining Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance, Wasserstein-1 distance, feature distance, variance ratio, event pattern similarity, and temporal proximity, and (iii) uses the resulting calibrated demand prior to drive both an LP-based fleet repositioning policy and batch dispatch with Hungarian matching. In ablation, a distributional-only metric subset achieves the strongest mean-wait reduction, while the full ensemble is retained as a robustness-oriented default that preserves calendar and event context.
  Evaluated on 5.2 million NYC TLC trips across 8 diverse scenarios (winter/summer, weekday/weekend/holiday, morning/evening/night) with 5 random seeds each, our method reduces mean rider wait times by 31.1% (bootstrap 95% CI: [26.5, 36.6]; Friedman chi-squared = 80.0, p = 4.25e-18; Cohen's d = 7.5-29.9). P95 wait drops 37.6% and the Gini coefficient of wait times improves from 0.441 to 0.409. The two contributions compose multiplicatively: calibration provides 16.9% reduction relative to the replay baseline; LP repositioning adds a further 15.5%. The approach requires no training, is deterministic and explainable, generalizes to Chicago (23.3% wait reduction using the NYC-built regime library without retraining), and is robust across fleet sizes (32-47% improvement for 0.5x-2.0x fleet scaling). Code is available at https://github.com/IndarKarhana/regime-calibrated-dispatch.

</details>


### [189] [Provable Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning: A Representation Learning Framework with Low Rank Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03891)
*Yaoze Guo,Shana Moothedath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多任务强化学习中的表示学习，提出了一种基于低秩矩阵估计的方法来学习跨任务的共享表示，能够在更一般的特征分布假设下实现准确的低秩矩阵恢复，并构建近似最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务强化学习中学习跨任务的共享表示面临挑战，因为RL数据的复杂性和策略依赖性导致误差的时间累积。现有方法依赖限制性假设（如高斯特征、不相干条件或最优解访问），需要更通用的方法处理RL环境中遇到的特征分布。

Method: 采用无奖励强化学习框架先学习数据收集策略，该策略指导探索策略来估计未知奖励矩阵。提出低秩矩阵估计方法，在更一般的特征分布假设下操作，不依赖高斯特征、不相干条件或最优解访问等限制性假设。

Result: 理论分析表明在放松的假设下可实现准确的低秩矩阵恢复，并刻画了表示误差与样本复杂度之间的关系。利用学习到的表示构建近似最优策略并证明遗憾界。实验结果表明该方法能有效从有限数据中学习鲁棒的共享表示和任务动态。

Conclusion: 该方法为多任务强化学习的表示学习提供了更通用的框架，能够在更现实的假设下学习共享表示，支持近似最优策略的构建，并在理论和实验上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Multi-task representation learning (MTRL) is an approach that learns shared latent representations across related tasks, facilitating collaborative learning that improves the overall learning efficiency. This paper studies MTRL for multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), where multiple tasks have the same state-action space and transition probabilities, but different rewards. We consider T linear Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) where the reward functions and transition dynamics admit linear feature embeddings of dimension d. The relatedness among the tasks is captured by a low-rank structure on the reward matrices. Learning shared representations across multiple RL tasks is challenging due to the complex and policy-dependent nature of data that leads to a temporal progression of error. Our approach adopts a reward-free reinforcement learning framework to first learn a data-collection policy. This policy then informs an exploration strategy for estimating the unknown reward matrices. Importantly, the data collected under this well-designed policy enable accurate estimation, which ultimately supports the learning of an near-optimal policy. Unlike existing approaches that rely on restrictive assumptions such as Gaussian features, incoherence conditions, or access to optimal solutions, we propose a low-rank matrix estimation method that operates under more general feature distributions encountered in RL settings. Theoretical analysis establishes that accurate low-rank matrix recovery is achievable under these relaxed assumptions, and we characterize the relationship between representation error and sample complexity. Leveraging the learned representation, we construct near-optimal policies and prove a regret bound. Experimental results demonstrate that our method effectively learns robust shared representations and task dynamics from finite data.

</details>


### [190] [Improving Model Performance by Adapting the KGE Metric to Account for System Non-Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03906)
*M Jawad,HV Gupta,YH Wang,MA Farmani,A Behrangi,GY Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JKGE_ss指标，用于检测和考虑水文数据中的动态非平稳性，相比传统NSE和KGE_ss指标能更好地评估模型在变化条件下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球科学系统通常具有显著的时间非平稳性，源于水文气象驱动因素的季节和气候变化，以及自然和人为的土地利用变化。传统模型评估指标（如NSE和KGE_ss）隐含地假设数据生成过程是时间平稳的，这一假设在地球科学中通常不成立，可能导致在变化条件下对模型性能的误导性评估。

Method: 引入JKGE_ss指标（基于KGE_ss改进），该指标能够检测并考虑数据统计特性中的动态非平稳性。与使用长期均值作为基准的NSE和KGE_ss不同，JKGE_ss强调系统存储时间变化的再现。通过在不同水文气候条件下（从近期降水主导到雪主导再到强干旱）训练物理概念和数据驱动的流域尺度模型来测试新指标的稳健性。

Result: 在所有测试案例中，使用JKGE_ss指标都改善了系统时间动态在所有时间尺度上的再现，包括从湿润到干旱年份以及整个流量范围（特别是退水期）。相比传统指标，JKGE_ss能更好地捕捉系统动态的时间变化。

Conclusion: 由于传统指标未能充分考虑系统动态的时间变化，可能在变化条件下导致对模型性能的误导性评估，建议在地球科学模型开发中采用JKGE_ss指标。

Abstract: Geoscientific systems tend to be characterized by pronounced temporal non-stationarity, arising from seasonal and climatic variability in hydrometeorological drivers, and from natural and anthropogenic changes to land use and cover. As has been pointed out, such variability renders "the assumption of statistical stationarity obsolete in water management", and requires us to "account for, rather than ignore, non-stationary trends" in the data. However, metrics used for model development are typically based on the implicit and unjustifiable assumption that the data generating process is time-stationary.
  Here, we introduce the JKGE_ss metric (adapted from KGE_ss) that detects and accounts for dynamical non-stationarity in the statistical properties of the data and thereby improves information extraction and model performance. Unlike NSE and KGE_ss, which use the long-term mean as a benchmark against which to evaluate model efficiency, JKGE_ss emphasizes reproduction of temporal variations in system storage.
  We tested the robustness of the new metric by training physical-conceptual and data-based catchment-scale models of varying complexity across a wide range of hydroclimatic conditions, from recent-precipitation-dominated to snow-dominated to strongly arid. In all cases, the result was improved reproduction of system temporal dynamics at all time scales, across wet to dry years, and over the full range of flow levels (especially recession periods).
  Since traditional metrics fail to adequately account for temporal shifts in system dynamics, potentially resulting in misleading assessments of model performance under changing conditions, we recommend the adoption of JKGE_ss for geoscientific model development.

</details>


### [191] [Align Your Structures: Generating Trajectories with Structure Pretraining for Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03911)
*Aniketh Iyengar,Jiaqi Han,Pengwei Sun,Mingjian Jiang,Jianwen Xie,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种利用结构预训练生成分子动力学轨迹的新框架，通过扩散模型生成结构，再通过插值器模块保证时间一致性，有效解决了MD数据稀缺和高维建模的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子动力学轨迹生成面临两大挑战：1）MD数据稀缺，难以获取大量轨迹数据；2）高维MD分布建模复杂。现有深度生成模型难以有效应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1）在大规模构象数据集上训练基于扩散的结构生成模型；2）在MD轨迹数据上训练插值器模块，确保生成结构的时间一致性。该方法将复杂MD建模分解为结构生成和时间对齐两个可管理子问题。

Result: 在QM9和DRUGS小分子数据集上进行了无条件生成、正向模拟和插值任务的全面评估，并扩展到四肽和蛋白质单体系统。实验结果显示，在几何、动力学和能量测量方面均取得显著精度提升，能生成化学真实的MD轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架通过利用丰富的结构数据缓解MD轨迹数据稀缺问题，并将复杂MD建模分解为两个可管理子问题，成功生成了高质量的化学真实MD轨迹，为分子动力学模拟提供了有效的新方法。

Abstract: Generating molecular dynamics (MD) trajectories using deep generative models has attracted increasing attention, yet remains inherently challenging due to the limited availability of MD data and the complexities involved in modeling high-dimensional MD distributions. To overcome these challenges, we propose a novel framework that leverages structure pretraining for MD trajectory generation. Specifically, we first train a diffusion-based structure generation model on a large-scale conformer dataset, on top of which we introduce an interpolator module trained on MD trajectory data, designed to enforce temporal consistency among generated structures. Our approach effectively harnesses abundant structural data to mitigate the scarcity of MD trajectory data and effectively decomposes the intricate MD modeling task into two manageable subproblems: structural generation and temporal alignment. We comprehensively evaluate our method on the QM9 and DRUGS small-molecule datasets across unconditional generation, forward simulation, and interpolation tasks, and further extend our framework and analysis to tetrapeptide and protein monomer systems.
